## enclave.100a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.100a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]星系中潜藏着什么东西：从时空织构中裂出的一个隐秘通道，它的视界泛着灵能的涟漪。探测器的数据飘忽不定，我们的科研船的船员报告了奇怪的干扰：内心深处藏着疯狂的幻境，一种被持续监视的感觉，断断续续的幻觉……\n\n尽管我们很可惜地缺乏有效的遥测手段，但似乎很显然，我们偶然发现了一条通向未知事物的隧道。这是一条危险的路径，但回报可能非常丰厚；在隧道的另一端则连接着星系中一个非常遥远的角落。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:23`

### English

Something waits in the [From.GetName] system: a veiled passage, cut from the fabric of spacetime, its horizon rippling with psionic energy. Sensor data is erratic, and the crew of our science vessel reports strange disturbances: wildly visceral dreamlike visions, a persistent sense of being watched, fitful hallucinations...\n\nDespite the frustrating lack of useful telemetry, it seems clear we have stumbled on a tunnel through uncharted reaches. A dangerous path, yet the rewards may be great; on the tunnel's far side lies a distant corner of the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:26`

## enclave.100b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.100b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]星系中潜藏着什么东西：从时空织构中裂出的一个隐秘通道，它的视界泛着灵能的涟漪。探测器的数据飘忽不定，但蜂巢已经被扰动了。一种被监视的感觉始终萦绕着我们，这是一种独特的感觉，似乎某物正在隧道的大口另一侧向我们袭来。\n\n尽管我们很可惜地缺乏有效的遥测手段，但似乎很显然，我们偶然发现了一条通向未知事物的隧道。这是一条危险的路径，但回报可能非常丰厚；在隧道的另一端则连接着星系中一个非常遥远的角落。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:24`

### English

Something waits in the [From.GetName] system: a veiled passage, cut from the fabric of spacetime, its horizon rippling with psionic energy. Sensor data is erratic, but the hive has been disturbed. A persistent feeling of being watched pervades us, the distinct sense that something looms just beyond the tunnel's mouth.\n\nDespite the frustrating lack of useful telemetry, it seems clear we have stumbled on a passage through uncharted reaches. A dangerous path, yet the rewards may be great; on the tunnel's far side lies a distant corner of the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:27`

## enclave.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们正在讨论是否要协助[fleet.GetName]建立一个独立的§Y佣兵城邦§!。这项提议最先是由舰队司令提出，并且如果通过，将会导致我们失去对舰队的控制权。我们还将负责向他们提供一笔可观的§Y能量币§!和§Y合金§!，不过作为回报，他们会给我们一些公司的股份，这将带来定期红利和特权。\n\n他们保证，一旦我们需要，他们将随时准备为我们而战，当然也为我们的钱也可以。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:28`

### English

We are debating whether or not to help set up the [fleet.GetName] as an independent §YMercenary Enclave§!. The proposal, first put forward by the fleet commanders, would result in us losing control of the fleet. We would also be responsible for providing them with a sizeable investment in §YEnergy Credits§! and §YAlloys§!. In return, they would give us a number of shares in the company. These would come with periodic dividends and special privileges.\n\nThey assure us that in times of need, they will always be ready to fight for us. And our money.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:32`

## enclave.25.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.25.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我方舰队已经成功瓦解了位于[merc_system.GetName]星系中佣兵们的抵抗，他们的基地也一并四分五裂。随着剩余的[FROM.GetName]相继崩溃，他们的残留舰队也随之解散。尽管部分还活着的舰长们沦落为海盗，但另一些则是宣称他们将计划继续提供一些小型合约服务，像是提供后勤支持或者是保卫空间站。但这样的小事无关紧要，重要的是，佣兵城邦已经不复存在，我们能够从它的残骸中打捞到相当数量的资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:46`

### English

Our fleet has overcome the defenses of the mercenaries in [merc_system.GetName], and their station has been blown to pieces. The rest of the [FROM.GetName] collapsed quickly after, and their remaining fleets have disbanded. While some of the surviving captains may turn to piracy, others have announced their plans to honor smaller contracts, offering logistical support or space station garrisoning. But such trifling affairs are of little consequence. The important fact is that the Mercenary Enclave is no more, and from its wreckage we have been able to salvage a sizable amount of resources.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:50`

## enclave.26.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.26.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有消息传来，[mercenary_destroyer.GetName]日前在[merc_system.GetName]星系中摧毁了一个佣兵城邦。由于进攻者过于强大，[FROM.GetName]独木难支，他们的董事会，兵营以及船坞均被摧毁。不久后佣兵舰队也随之解散，因为各位舰长都只能自谋生路了，但其中一部分代表声称他们仍将尽力履行公司在后勤以及恒星基地防卫的合约。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:47`

### English

Word has reached us that a Mercenary Enclave has been destroyed by the [mercenary_destroyer.GetName] in [merc_system.GetName]. The aggressors proved too strong for the [FROM.GetName] to face on their own, and the station housing the Board, barracks, and their shipyards was destroyed. The mercenary fleet disbanded shortly after, as every captain was left to fend for themselves, but some representatives have stated that they will honor the company's contracts in logistics and starbase garrisoning as best they can despite the dissolution of the Company.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:51`

## enclave.26.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.26.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他们会为此付出代价。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:48`

### English

They will pay for this.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:52`

## enclave.27

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.27&lang=en)

### 中文

舰队紧急召回

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:49`

### English

Emergency Fleet Recall

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:53`

## enclave.27.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.27.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尊敬的客户：\n\n由于目前遭受攻击，我们迫切需要召回自己的舰队。根据合同第285b条，我们的舰队将在收到通知后立即停止为您服务。不过一旦我们将这一不幸的局势处理完毕，我们就将恢复我们的合作关系，并对由此带来的不便表示歉意。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:50`

### English

Dear valued client,\n\nAs we currently find ourselves under attack, we have urgent need to recall our own forces. As per our contract, we will immediately enact clause 285b.15, and our fleet will leave your service as soon as this notice reaches you. We look forward to renewing our partnership once this unfortunate situation is resolved, and apologize for any inconvenience.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:54`

## enclave.27.desc.mindwarden

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.27.desc.mindwarden&lang=en)

### 中文

刚刚我们收到了[mercenary_enclave_country.GetName]发来的讯息：\n\n§H[root.GetName]，\n\n我们目前正遭受攻击，必须立刻召回己方武装。根据合约相关条款，[mercenary_fleet.GetName]将即刻停止为您服务。\n\n我们相信，您能够理解当前我方局势之紧迫与召回武装之必要。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:51`

### English

We have received communication from the [mercenary_enclave_country.GetName]:\n\n§H[root.GetName],\n\nWe are under attack, and must therefore recall our own forces immediately. In accordance with our contract, the [mercenary_fleet.GetName] will leave your service forthwith.\n\nWe trust that you will understand the urgency of this situation and the necessity of our actions.§!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:55`

## enclave.28

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.28&lang=en)

和平协定

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:56`

### English

Peace Deal

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:60`

## enclave.28.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.28.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

注意了！[Root.GetName]，距离你们发动这场令人遗憾的侵略已经过去整整一年了。我们，[mercenary_enclave_country.GetName]，愿意不计前嫌；不管怎样，冲突尽管有时候是有利可图的，但如果是两位生意伙伴之间发生冲突的话那就不可能了。如果你们愿意简明地发表一份致歉声明，向我们传达出些许的善意，我们愿意既往不咎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:57`

### English

Attention, [Root.GetName], a year has passed since your recent regrettable act of aggression. We of the [mercenary_enclave_country.GetName] are ready to let bygones be bygones - after all, conflict may be profitable, but not when it comes between business partners. If you would simply offer an apology, and send us a modest token of goodwill, we are willing to overlook this unfortunate incident.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:61`

### English

## enclave.205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

欢迎！[mercenary_enclave_country.GetName]向我们的客户提供最尖端的安全服务。我们与许许多多星球签订了民间协约，包括个人安保或执法部门训练。\n\n不过对于我们的高级客户，我们致力于提供最极致的服务。舰队、陆军、军事顾问……你可以有许多用于维系和平的选项。当然，价格合理。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:61`

### English

Welcome! [mercenary_enclave_country.GetName] offers state of the art security services to our customers. We have civil contracts across multiple worlds, ranging from personal security details to training drills for enforcers.\n\nBut for our high profile clients, we aim to provide the best of the best. Fleets, armies, military advisors... A range of peacekeeping options is available. For a modest fee of course.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:65`

## enclave.205.desc.hostile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.205.desc.hostile&lang=en)

### 中文

啊，战争贩子回来了。\n\n你终于想清楚你这些毫无意义的行为是徒劳的了吗？这是一点小意思，谨表对你善意的回馈，我们[mercenary_enclave_country.GetName]愿意将这些分歧暂时搁置。让我们一起期待一个有利可图的未来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:62`

### English

Ah, the warmonger returns.\n\nHave you finally come to your senses and seen the futility of these pointless actions of yours? For a small sum, a mere token of your goodwill, we at the [mercenary_enclave_country.GetName] are willing to put these disagreements aside. Together, let us look forward to a profitable future.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:66`

## enclave.210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

棒极了！招募一支[mercenary_enclave_country.GetName]舰队永远是正确的选择。我们的部队绝对是星系中最训练有素、纪律严明的，而我们的科技也处于绝对尖端。海盗、叛军或者其他需要你处理的乌合之众会很快学会什么叫尊重。\n\n此外，我们的每一支舰队都由一位身经百战的舰队指挥官，这是不需要额外付费的。他们知悉星际战争的方方面面，一定可以带领你的军队走向胜利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:86`

### English

Excellent! Recruiting a [mercenary_enclave_country.GetName] fleet is always the right choice. Our forces are amongst the best-trained and most disciplined in the galaxy, and our tech is cutting edge. Pirates, insurgents, and any other rabble you might have recourse to deal with will soon learn to show respect.\n\n Additionally, each of our fleets comes with a battle-proven Commander at no extra charge. Adept in all facets of interstellar warfare, they will surely lead your forces to victory.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:91`

## enclave.211.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.211.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我相信你一定会运用好你的新部队，而和平将很快回到你的帝国！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:87`

### English

I trust that you will put your new troops to excellent use, and that peace will soon return to your empire!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:92`

## enclave.215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你是想终止还是延长合同？我们得先准备必要的文件。目前我方正在开展一项促销活动，续签合同的客户将收到一箱免费手榴弹作为赠礼！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:88`

### English

Do you wish to terminate or prolong a contract? Let us prepare the necessary documents. We are currently running a promotion where customers receive a complimentary crate of grenades for every renewed contract!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:93`

## enclave.215.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.215.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们想讨论其他事务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:89`

### English

We'd like to discuss another matter.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:94`

## enclave.215.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.215.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们想延长协约。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:90`

### English

We wish to extend our contract.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:95`

## enclave.215.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.215.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们想终止协约。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:91`

### English

We wish to terminate our contract.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:96`

## enclave.220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的驻防部队能够表现出非凡的价值，但是请牢记，在[mercenary_enclave_country.GetName]我们避讳用“雇佣军”这个词称呼他们。这个词太直白，带有太多的负面联想。我们的“国防承包商”已经准备好对你的空间站进行布置，以迎接这些“安全专家”。在我们就绪后，这些专家将开始训练空间站的成员，以使他们发挥自己最好的水平。\n\n在[mercenary_enclave_country.GetName]，我们只专心训练最杰出的和平卫士。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:92`

### English

Our garrisons offer exceptional value. However, please keep in mind that at [mercenary_enclave_country.GetName] we frown upon the term 'mercenary.' The word is too blunt, and carries far too many negative associations. Our 'defense contractors' are standing by to prepare your station for the arrival of our 'safety specialists.' Once we're on site, these specialists will begin drilling the station crew to bring out their best. \n\n At [mercenary_enclave_country.GetName] we aim to train only the finest peacekeepers.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:97`

## enclave.225.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.225.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

感谢你的生意。与你合作维护星系的安全，这让我们倍感骄傲。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:93`

### English

Thank you for your business. We are proud to work with you to make the galaxy a safer place.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:98`

## enclave.235.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.235.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有了这些资金，我们可以极大地扩大我们的行动。更多的舰船，更多的人员，更加庞大、完善的训练设施……我们可以在维护和平这方面做的更多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:94`

### English

With these funds, we could greatly expand our operation. More ships, more personnel, bigger and better training facilities... We would be able to do so much more to help keep the peace.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:100`

## enclave.235.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.235.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在这样一个诡谲多变、纷繁复杂的星系，安全考虑是至高无上的。你的捐赠能让我们的行动和服务更上一层楼！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:95`

### English

In an ever-changing and complex galaxy, security concerns are paramount. Your donation would allow us to scale up our operations and services !

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:101`

## enclave.235.3.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.235.3.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

想象一下：[mercenary_enclave_country.GetName]为了各类民事和军事行动部署在整个星系……对所有人来说，前所未有的安全感……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:96`

### English

Just imagine: [mercenary_enclave_country.GetName] deployed all across the galaxy for civilian and military operation... An unprecedented level of security for all...

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:102`

## enclave.235.4.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.235.4.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们开始这一切的时候，我们成立者中的一位怀揣着梦想：我们的存在是为星系带来安全，正如寰宇企业的存在是为了消灭不平等。今天，我很兴奋地宣布我们的这一目标已经触手可及。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:97`

### English

When we started this, one of our founders had a dream: to do for galactic security what megacorps have done for inequality. Today, I am excited to say that this goal is within our reach.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:103`

## enclave.235.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.235.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

*我们会在整个星系部署最为庞大的舰队！资金会流动起来。平民们会安享他们应得的稳定。国家会为我们的合同大打出手……噢，当我告诉股东们新闻时，真该看看他们的脸！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:98`

### English

*We will field the LARGEST fleet in the galaxy! Credits will flow. Civilians will finally enjoy the stability they deserve. Nations will fight for our contracts... Oh the face of our shareholders when I tell them the news!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:104`

## enclave.240.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.240.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

是啊。对所有人来说这些都是令人忧心忡忡的时刻。我们的战略问题解决团队在创建更为安全的星系方面有着坚定不移的道德导向和毫不动摇的奉献精神。我们冒着极大的危险近距离监测新出现的危险事物，并且可以为你提供赖以行动的情报。你想要的是……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:99`

### English

Yes. These are concerning times for all. Our team of strategic problem solvers has a steadfast moral compass and unwavering dedication to creating a safer galaxy. We took great risks to closely monitor the emergence of new perils and can provide you with actionable intel about...

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:106`

## enclave.240.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.240.a&lang=en)

### 中文

大可汗

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:100`

### English

The Great Khan.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:107`

灰蛊风暴

### English

The Gray Tempest

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:108`

### English

The Prethoryn Swarm

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:109`

异次元入侵者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:103`

### English

The Extradimensional Invaders

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:110`

## enclave.240.e

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.240.e&lang=en)

### 中文

肃正协议

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:104`

### English

The Contingency AI

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:111`

## enclave.240.f

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.240.f&lang=en)

### 中文

塞塔娜

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:105`

### English

Cetana

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:112`

## enclave.245.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.245.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

可汗的舰船总体上结构完善，没有明确弱点。我们的分析人士建议称鱼雷或许可以较好地击落他们的舰船，但要留意避免被针对防御。把护盾和装甲结合起来大概是保护你们舰船的最佳策略。可汗已然是极有能力的指挥官，低估他会是严重的错误。要把可汗的亲卫视作莫大的威胁才对。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:106`

### English

The Khan's ships present an overall well rounded composition, with no clear weakness. Our analysts suggest that torpedoes might perform well to take their ships down, but caution against specialized defense. A mix of both shields and armor is probably your best bet to keep your ships alive. The Khan has proven to be an extremely competent commander and underestimating them would be a terrible mistake. Consider their retinue as an extreme menace.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:114`

## enclave.250.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.250.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

灰蛊风暴依赖两类互为补充的武器：能量束和舰载机。前者受护盾反制，但护盾会被舰载机绕过。我们的预测模型显示在护盾的基础上安装点防御可以大大提升舰船在交战中生还的概率。纳米舰船的防护极佳，但鱼雷或许能突破它们的防御。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:107`

### English

The Gray Tempest relies on two complementary weapon types: energy beams and strike craft. The former is countered by shields, but shields are bypassed by their strike craft. Our projections show that ships fitted with point defense in addition to shields have a much better chance to survive the engagement. Nanite ships are extremely well protected, but torpedoes might help in breaching their defenses.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:115`

## enclave.255.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.255.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

索林原虫舰队靠着速度缓慢的动能武器和大群小型舰船打垮敌人。选项之一是用点防御御敌，结合以装甲和提升过的船体恢复力；另一个选项是远距离交战。使用足够强力的能量武器，你可以做到在敌人进入其攻击距离前就摧毁它们的舰队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:108`

### English

Prethoryn fleets rely on slow moving projectiles and hosts of smaller ships to take down their foes. One option to fend them off is to use point defense, coupled with armor and improvements to your ship's hull resilience; another is to engage them at extreme range. With sufficiently powerful energy weapons, one might manage to destroy their fleets before they can even get in range.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:116`

## enclave.260.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.260.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

破界者单纯靠能量战斗是我们可以利用的明显弱点。能对其护盾造成额外伤害的武器都会有摧枯拉朽的效果，不过如果装上可以完全绕过护盾的武器的话，它们在这个次元同样活不了多久。就防御而言，建议在战斗中带好护盾，以充分反制敌人的能量束。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:109`

### English

The Unbidden's exclusive reliance on energy gives us an obvious weakness to exploit. Weapons dealing extra damage to their shields should prove devastating, but if you can fit weapons that completely bypass their shields, they won't be long for this dimension. Defensively, you should look at bringing your own shields to the fight to adequately counter their energy beams.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:117`

## enclave.265.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.265.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

肃正协议的舰船单纯使用能量武器——如此一来，最好用护盾降低承受的伤害。模拟结果表明尽管肃正协议舰船防御很高，但其船体相对脆弱。我们的分析人士估计称穿透性武器可能会在与肃正协议的战斗中表现出色。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:110`

### English

Contingency ships exclusively use energy weapons- as such, it is best to use shields to mitigate incoming damage. Simulations indicate that despite having strong defense, their ship's hulls are relatively fragile. Our analysts estimate that penetrating weapons might perform very well against them.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:118`

## enclave.266.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.266.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

塞塔娜的舰船依赖于远距离、高伤害的能量武器和坚固的再生装甲。他们擅长击落大型目标，所以你最好用成群的小型飞行器骚扰他们。鱼雷或能量武器应该足以对抗她的舰队，为你的舰船装备足够的护盾可以让他们生存更长时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:111`

### English

Cetana's ships rely on long range, high damage energy weapons and sturdy regenerating armor. They are adept at taking down large targets, and you would do well to instead harass them with swarms of smaller crafts. Torpedoes or energy weapons should perform adequately against her fleets, and shielding your vessels might help them survive a little longer.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:119`

## enclave.270.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.270.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

打算平定某个殖民地或是根除恐怖分子？我们的行星际支援服务能够成建制地提供训练有素的专业人士，他们时刻准备着在具有挑战性的高风险环境下工作，帮助您为星系带来秩序。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:112`

### English

Looking to pacify a colony or uproot terrorists? Our planetside support services offer companies of trained professionals, ready to work in challenging and high-risk environments to help you bring order to the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:121`

## enclave.270.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.270.a&lang=en)

### 中文

一小支佣兵

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:113`

### English

A small contingent of mercenaries.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:122`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asmall_merc_army_hire&lang=en)

### 中文

这些佣兵受到无限期雇佣，但维护费高昂。此军队包括§Y5支佣兵陆军§! 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:114`

### English

These mercenaries are hired indefinitely but have a high upkeep. This force includes §Y5 Mercenary Armies§! 

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:123`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:124`

### English

These mercenaries are hired indefinitely but have a high upkeep. This force includes §Y5 Mechanized Mercenary Armies§!

## enclave.270.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.270.c&lang=en)

### 中文

一支可观的佣兵部队。

### English

A sizeable troop of mercenaries.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:126`

## medium_merc_army_hire

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amedium_merc_army_hire&lang=en)

### 中文

这些佣兵受到无限期雇佣，但维护费高昂。此军队包括§Y10支佣兵陆军§! 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:118`

### English

These mercenaries are hired indefinitely but have a high upkeep. This force includes §Y10 Mercenary Armies§! 

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:127`

## enclave.270.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.270.d&lang=en)

### 中文

## enclave.280.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.280.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尊敬的赞助人！您今天来此有何贵干？您看过我们最新的船只设计图了吗？它们将是我们为民用客户提供的全新“个人安全”服务的核心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:123`

### English

Esteemed patron! What business brings you to us today? Have you seen our latest ship schematics? They will be the core of our new 'Personal Security' offer for civilian customers.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:133`

## enclave.280.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.280.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

能够招待你是何等的荣幸！我们已经准备了一些小食，希望我们在召集董事会的时候，这些小食能够聊表敬意。如果你将注意力转到窗户，我会看到我们的一支维和部队正在进行演练。训练的假人代表平民。请注意他们是如何将平民召集起来，并妥善安排的。在军事上一丝不苟，实在是奇迹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:124`

### English

What a pleasure to receive you! We have prepared some refreshments, should it please you to sample them while we summon the Board. If you direct your attention to the window, you'll see one of our peacekeeping divisions engaged in maneuvers. The training dummies represent civilians. Note how efficiently they round them up and handle them. A marvel of military precision.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:134`

## enclave.280.c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.280.c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们一直以来都很乐意同赞助人会面。请放心，下一批红利很快就会发到你的手中。你介不介意和我们聊一聊，你对于我们在严重后果保护领域展开的新业务的范围有什么看法？我们觉得民用客户肯定会爱死这些服务了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:125`

### English

We are always delighted to meet our patrons. Rest assured that the next dividend shipment should reach you shortly. Would you care to tell us what you think about our range of new services in high consequence protection? We think our civilian clients will love them!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:135`

## enclave.280.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.280.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们想让你§Y召回§!你的舰队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:126`

### English

We want you to §Yrecall§! your fleet.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:136`

## enclave.280.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.280.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们乐于同你分享我们的§Y科技§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:127`

### English

We are inclined to share our §Ytechnology§! with you.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:137`

## enclave.280.c.timeout

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.280.c.timeout&lang=en)

### 中文

这个城邦还在利用我们的科技进行升级。我们可以在§Y[event_target:mercenary_enclave_country.updating_databanks_timer]§!之后与他们分享新的科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:128`

### English

The enclave is still upgrading with our tech. We can share new technologies with them after §Y[event_target:mercenary_enclave_country.updating_databanks_timer]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:138`

## enclave.285.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.285.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你应该明白召回舰队是一件相当敏感的事情。当然了，我们已经在所有合同模板中准备好了条款漏洞，不过我们至少需要六个月的时间来调遣舰队回来，而且还需要支付一大笔补偿以避免惹恼现有客户。\n\n我们认为这一行动不符合我们组织的最佳利益，但您是我们的赞助人——如果您愿意，我们愿意效劳。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:129`

### English

You certainly understand that recalling a fleet is a sensitive matter. We have of course prepared the necessary loopholes in all our standard contracts, but we will need at least six months to bring the fleet back, and a... generous compensation to avoid upsetting our current customers.\n\nWe believe that this course of action is not in the best interests of our organization, but you are our patron - we'll make it happen if you wish it so.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:139`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.294.name&lang=en)

### 中文

停工通知

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:133`

### English

Termination Notice

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:143`

## enclave.294.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.294.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

亲爱的客户，\n\n依照我们的合约中第285b.15条款，我们必须遗憾地通知你，我们计划提早关闭相关服务，并收回你从我们这里租借的舰队。依照合同的惯常条例：这份通知将提前三个月送达你处作为提醒，然后我们才会让舰队停止向你提供服务。此外，我们会向你提供应得的丰厚补偿金。\n\n我们为造成的不便感到抱歉，并希望你在未来的打拼中能够继续拥有好运气。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:134`

### English

Dearest client,\n\nAs provisioned by clause 285b.15 of our contract, we must regrettably bring our arrangement to an early close and recall the Mercenary Fleet that you rented from us. As per contract stipulations: this notice serves as your 3-month warning before the fleet must leave your service. We enclose the generous compensation to which you are entitled.\n\nWe apologize for the inconvenience, and wish you the best of luck in your future endeavors.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:144`

## enclave.294.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.294.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们亟需这些舰船！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:135`

### English

We needed those ships!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:145`

## enclave.2030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的后勤服务能够帮助你部署舰队，到达星系的最边缘处。我们干练的专家将确保你的舰船配备了所有需要的东西，无论是弹药还是食物，甚至精确到你的温度管理器所需的铆钉。此外，我们会在舰队经过之处提前部署补给仓库，或者同当地的民用公司签订配给合同。你的舰船值得最高级的维护，而我们就是为此提供服务的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:136`

### English

Our logistic support service is there to help you deploy your fleets to the farthest reaches of the galaxy. Our dedicated experts will make sure that your ships are equipped with all they need, from ammunition to food, down to the last bolt on your temperature regulators. Additionally, we will set up supply depots ahead of the fleet, or broker provisioning contracts with local civilian companies. Your ships deserve the best care, and we are here to provide it.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:147`

## enclave.2035.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2035.name&lang=en)

### 中文

终止后勤支援协约

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:137`

### English

Logistic Support Contract Ending

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:148`

## enclave.2035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

亲爱的客户，\n\n我们向你提供的后勤支援服务已经到期，需要续约了。根据我们合约的条款：如果我们没有另外收到相关指示，你方将自动续约这项服务，并被收取相关费用。\n\n我们相信你对我们的服务很满意，也期待能和你在业务上展开更多的合作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:138`

### English

Dearest client,\n\nThe Logistic Support service we provide you is due for renewal. As per the terms of our contract: if we do not receive prompt instructions to do otherwise, you will be automatically billed for renewal of this service.\n\nWe trust that you are satisfied, and look forward to doing more business with you!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:149`

## enclave_mercenary_starbase_ruined_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave_mercenary_starbase_ruined_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个一度荣光的商业雇佣兵堡垒现在已经沦为断壁残垣；它的展厅遭到劫掠，所有价值连城的财富都被洗劫一空。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:173`

### English

This once-proud fortress of mercenary commerce has fallen to ruin; its showrooms have been looted of all their pitiless treasures.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:191`

## enclave_salvager_starbase_ruined_desc

### 中文

## enclave_shroudwalker_starbase_ruined_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave_shroudwalker_starbase_ruined_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个曾经为庇护所的地方被撕得四分五裂，看上去撕裂是从里面发生的；它的岩石支柱现在变得粉碎，废墟中无数尖顶被抛到宇宙之风中，散落得到处都是。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:185`

### English

This former refuge has been torn asunder, seemingly from within. Its rocky backbone now lies shattered, casting the ruin's myriad spires to the cosmic winds.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:203`

## mercenary_army

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amercenary_army&lang=en)

### 中文

佣兵陆军

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:186`

### English

Mercenary Army

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:205`

## mercenary_army_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amercenary_army_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些部队为了钱驻扎在此。他们冷酷而高效，但尽管其工作蕴含着风险，他们并不情愿为了某一目的献出生命。而且也决不会放过劫掠的机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:187`

### English

These troops are in it for the money. They are ruthless and efficient, but while risk is part of the job, they are unwilling to sacrifice themselves for a cause. And will never pass on a chance to pillage.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:206`

## mecha_army

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amecha_army&lang=en)

### 中文

机械化佣兵陆军

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:188`

### English

Mechanized Mercenary Army

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:207`

## mecha_army_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amecha_army_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

市面上最好的佣兵。这些部队驾驶着行动自如的巨型机械，足以弥补其数量不足的缺陷。尽管并非最勇敢的士兵，他们不会手下留情，身后只会留下硝烟未散的废墟。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:189`

### English

The best mercenaries on the market. These troops make up for their low numbers by piloting huge walking machines. Although they are not the bravest soldiers, they are merciless, and leave only smoldering ruins in their wake.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:208`

## salvager_overdrive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asalvager_overdrive&lang=en)

## salvager_overdrive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asalvager_overdrive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

受益于打捞者友邦船员专心致志的努力，该帝国得以加速建造恒星基地和殖民地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:191`

### English

This empire is benefitting from the focused efforts of a friendly Salvager crew, speeding up construction in their starbases and colonies.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:211`

## salvager_insight_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asalvager_insight_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

受益于打捞者城邦切合实际的专长，该帝国的科学家得以快速跟进工程中令人兴奋的新突破。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:193`

### English

This empire's scientists benefit from the practical expertise of a Salvager Enclave, fast-tracking exciting new breakthroughs in engineering.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:213`

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:201`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:223`

## enclave.2000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尊敬的赞助者，我们新近的合约有了丰硕的成果。我们扩展了自己的民用业务，现在有好几个公司雇佣我们，让我们提供安保和维和服务。在合约期间，我们的特工碰巧找到了一些有价值的科学研究。你会在定期分红的末尾找到这些附加的内容。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:202`

### English

Esteemed Patron, our recent contracts have been fruitful. We have expanded our civilian operations, and several corporations have hired us for a variety of security and peacekeeping services. While on contract, our operatives chanced upon some valuable scientific research. You will find it attached along with your regular dividends.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:224`

## enclave.2005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尊敬的赞助者，公司运营的很好，我们的收入一直在稳定的增加。以上是本次分红，此外我们还运来了一批额外的资源，这是我们从一个违约的民用客户手中扣押得到的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:203`

### English

Esteemed Patron, the company is doing well, and our earnings are steadily increasing. Here are your dividends, along with an extra delivery of resources we recovered from a civilian client who defaulted on their payment.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:225`

## enclave.2010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尊敬的赞助者，我们很高兴在这个财报周期宣告自己的利润突破了记录！鉴于此，我们很乐意在常规分红中加入一笔额外的丰厚提成。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:204`

### English

Esteemed Patron, we are pleased to announce record profits during this fiscal interval! As such, we have included a generous bonus to your regular dividends.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:226`

## enclave.2015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尊敬的赞助者，我们的船坞一直在毫不停歇地运转，生产质量最好的舰队。除了你的分红，我们也很高兴向你提供一支生产过剩的舰队，采用的是我们最新的设计，供你评估与测试。我们很乐意获得您的反馈！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:205`

### English

Esteemed Patron, our shipyards have been working tirelessly to produce the very best fleet possible. In addition to your dividends, we are pleased to offer you a complimentary fleet of our latest designs for you to review and test out. Let us know what you think of them!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:227`

## enclave.2020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尊敬的赞助者，我们最近的维和行动成功捣毁了几个海盗组织，我们收缴了一批数量极其丰厚的资源和科技数据。同你的常规分红一起，我们将这批战利品的大部分交付给你。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:206`

### English

Esteemed Patron, our latest peacekeeping operation had us dismantle several pirate operations and we recovered an extraordinary amount of resources and technological data. Most of these have been set apart for you, in addition to your well-earned dividends.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:228`

## enclave.2025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尊敬的赞助者，公司现在处于困难时期。我们失去了好几个民用客户，并且几个其他的行动也遭遇失败，损失惨重。我们设立了一个重组计划，将会削减董事会的开销，此外我们也会向社会输送一批人才，让我们的雇员灵活就业。\n\n请放心，我们会不知疲倦地将你的分红挣回来，这样的不幸只是暂时的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:207`

### English

Esteemed Patron, the company is going through difficult times. We lost several civilian clients, and various of our other operations ended in costly failures. We are setting up a restructuring plan that will cut expenditures across the board, and we will be letting some of our staff go.\n\nRest assured that we worked tirelessly to bring you your dividends, and that this misfortune is only temporary.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:229`

## enclave.2025.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2025.a&lang=en)

### 中文

令人失望。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:208`

### English

Disappointing.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:230`

## enclave.2025.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2025.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你被解雇了。去董事会领铺盖走人吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:209`

### English

You're fired. See the Board about your severance package.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:231`

## enclave.2025.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2025.c&lang=en)

### 中文

改让我们来帮忙吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:210`

### English

Let us help instead.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:232`

## enclave.2040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你想要分享自己的科技？亲爱的赞助人，你的信任让我们倍感荣幸。尽管我们的研发部门一直致力于让我们处于尖端科技水平，论及能够动用的资源总量，同你相比我们还是捉襟见肘。任何你同我们分享的知识都能让我们以最讲伦理的方式继续开展活动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:211`

### English

You want to share your technology? Your trust in us is an honor, dear patron. While our R&D department is committed to keeping us on the bleeding edge of research, we simply cannot mobilize the same amount of resources as you. And any knowledge that you share with us will be used to develop our activities in the most ethical manner!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:233`

## enclave.2040.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2040.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将同这个太空城邦分享自己的§Y科技§!。只能每§Y10年§!进行一次。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:212`

### English

We will share our §Ytechnology§! with this enclave. This can only be done once every §Y10 years§!.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:234`

## merc_enclave_grants_ships

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amerc_enclave_grants_ships&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[mercenary_enclave_country]§!还会额外给予我们一支§G小型军用舰队§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:213`

### English

§Y[mercenary_enclave_country]§! will additionally grant us a §Gsmall military fleet§!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:235`

## enclave.2050.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2050.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不应拒绝的交易

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:214`

### English

A Deal We Should Not Refuse

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:237`

## enclave.2050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到了来自[mercenary_enclave_country.GetName]的一份紧急公报。他们说[khan_country.GetName]提供给了他们一个不容许拒绝的提议。他们唯一的出路是在两个月内找到另外一位雇主。如果没有人雇佣他们，他们将不得不为大汗效力，在别无选择的情况下对我们发动全面战争。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:215`

### English

We have received an urgent communique from the [mercenary_enclave_country.GetName]. They say that the [khan_country.GetName] made them an offer they cannot refuse. Their only way out of the deal is to find employment elsewhere within two months. If no one hires them, they will enter the service of the Khan and have no choice but to wage total war against us.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:238`

## enclave.2050.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2050.a&lang=en)

### 中文

联系[mercenary_enclave_country.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:216`

### English

Contact the [mercenary_enclave_country.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:239`

## enclave.2050.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2050.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们有£time£§Y60天§!时间来决定我们是否雇佣他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:217`

### English

We have £time£ §Y60 Days§! to decide if we want to hire them or not.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:240`

## enclave.2050.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2050.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在负担不起。或许以后吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:218`

### English

We cannot afford it now. Maybe later.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:241`

## enclave.2050.b.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2050.b.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们有£time£§Y7天§!时间来决定我们是否雇佣他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:219`

### English

We have £time£ §Y7 Days§! to decide if we want to hire them or not.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:242`

## enclave.2050.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.2050.c&lang=en)

### 中文

叛徒！贪婪将使他们倾覆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:220`

### English

Traitors! Their greed will be their downfall.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:243`

## enclave.305.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.305.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[salvager_enclave_country.GetName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:222`

### English

[salvager_enclave_country.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:246`

## enclave.305.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.305.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

很高兴与您相识！我谨代表[salvager_enclave_country.GetName]，欢迎您莅临鄙邦简陋的星际工坊。\n\n诶呀…我在哪儿都能认出龙骨上的那些东西——制作的很精致。我们有着跟踪星系中飞船设计的习惯，试着同当地风潮保持一致。不过我刚刚讲到哪儿…？\n\n啊，对了：欢迎您！请问我们要如何满足您打捞和回收的需求？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:223`

### English

Pleased to make your acquaintance! On behalf of the [salvager_enclave_country.GetName], I welcome you to our humble, interstellar workshop.\n\nSay... I'd recognize those keel fixtures anywhere - some fine work. We make a habit of keeping up with what's flying in the galaxy, try to keep up with local trends. But where was I..?\n\nAh yes: welcome! How can we facilitate your salvage and reclamation needs?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:247`

## enclave.305.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.305.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

您好，您好！我谨代表[salvager_enclave_country.GetName]，欢迎您莅临鄙邦的维修场。不论船体、引擎、蓝图还是配件：我们都有存货。\n\n我可不是开玩笑——人们在老旧飞船里扔下的东西会让你大吃一惊的。最近我刚入[salvager_enclave_country.species.GetHandNamePlural]了一套完整的厨房单元，用黄金铸成的。爽到。\n\n扯远了，请问我们要如何满足您打捞和回收的需求？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:224`

### English

Hello, hello! On behalf of the [salvager_enclave_country.GetName], I welcome you to our salvage yard. Hulls, drives, blueprints, fittings: we stock it all.\n\nI'm not even joking - you'd be amazed what people leave inside their old ships. Just recently I laid my [salvager_enclave_country.species.GetHandNamePlural] on an entire galley unit, cast in gold. Wild.\n\nAnyway, how can we facilitate your salvage and reclamation needs?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:248`

## enclave.305.c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.305.c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

向您致以敬意！我谨代表[salvager_enclave_country.GetName]，欢迎您莅临鄙邦的工坊和废品场。大批专职工程师已经就绪，准备好维修、重建或是拆建几乎任何他们可以用工具鼓捣的东西。您会发觉我们对贸易的激情可谓永无止境。\n\n请问今天我们要如何满足您打捞和回收的需求？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:225`

### English

Salutations! On behalf of the [salvager_enclave_country.GetName], I welcome you to our workshop and scrapyard. Legions of dedicated engineers stand ready to repair, rebuild, and tear down just about anything they can latch a tool onto. You'll find our passion for the trade is quite insatiable.\n\nHow can we facilitate your salvage and reclamation needs today?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:249`

## enclave.305.desc.scavenger_first

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.305.desc.scavenger_first&lang=en)

### 中文

欢迎！我很乐意同一些打捞业的同行会面——要知道，星系这个地方对我们来说老寂寞了呢。\n\n你知道训练内容——我们将别人觉得没有任何用处的老旧部件收集到一起，然后赋予它们全新的生命。如果遇到任何用你的[Root.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural]搞不定的事情，放轻松，我们会过来接手的。\n\n那么，我们有什么能够为你的打捞和开荒事业提供便利的呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:226`

### English

Welcome! I sure am glad to meet some fellow scavengers - the galaxy can be a lonely place for those like us.\n\nYou know the drill - we gather together old parts that no one sees any use for, and breathe new life into them. If there's anything you can't get your [Root.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural] on, rest assured, we will have it.\n\nAnyway, how can we facilitate your salvage and reclamation needs?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:250`

## enclave.305.desc.scavenger_followup

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.305.desc.scavenger_followup&lang=en)

### 中文

向你问好！你是否对于运营一个帝国已经感到了厌烦？我可以加入我们的捡垃圾事业——完全没有那么多压力，你肯定可以玩得转的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:227`

### English

Greetings! Have you gotten bored of running an empire yet? You could join us in the scrapping business - it's much less stressful, and you clearly have the knack for it.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:251`

## enclave.305.n.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.305.n.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

很高兴又见面了，我们这里可是忙的很。贵国最近的各种事务处理的还好吗？我敢说我们现在还是一如既往的喧闹。空闲的[salvager_enclave_country.species.GetHandNamePlural]在处理过时的殖民船这方面可是很有一套的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:228`

### English

Hello again! Busy times, these - I trust your empire's affairs are running smoothly? I dare say we're all abuzz here, as usual. Idle [salvager_enclave_country.species.GetHandNamePlural] make fast work of outdated colony ships...

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:252`

## enclave.305.o.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.305.o.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

您好！我们今天又该怎样满足你们的工程需求呢？\n\n呃，你们船身钢板上安装的玩意儿挺有意思，它们用起来效果如何？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:229`

### English

Greetings! And how might we serve your engineering needs today?\n\nHmm, curious hull plate fixtures you have there - how are they working out for you?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:253`

## enclave.305.p.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.305.p.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

欢迎回来！嘿，来看看这些崭新丝滑的引擎怎么样吧！我真诚希望我们能对您维护舰船的方式做一些微小的贡献；它们会为此感谢您的。\n\n那么我们如何为您效劳？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:230`

### English

Welcome back! And hey, get a load of those smooth, new drive signatures! I do hope we're managing to impart some small, positive influence on the way you maintain your ships; they'll thank you for it.\n\nHow can we be of service?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:254`

## enclave.305.q.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.305.q.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你好啊！别介意……只是在对这个工业级的全息发射器进行一些修修补补。这些，非常令人捉迷的装置。这可是我的拿手项目，研究这玩意究竟能够投影一个多大的全息图像。这甚至不及你将其投影到太空中的一半困难，看见没。\n\n啊，我一开口能跑火车好几年呢。我该如何为你效劳？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:231`

### English

Hello! Don't mind me... Just been tinkering with this here industrial holo-emitter. Fascinating devices, these. It's a pet project of mine, to see how large a hologram it could actually project. Not half as difficult when you're beaming one out into space, see.\n\nAh, but I could prattle on for ages. How can we be of service?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:255`

## enclave.305.organic.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.305.organic.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

很高兴再次见到你！不要介意这些破铜烂铁，还有那些呻吟的声音。我们，呃……有朋自远方来！它们正在长身体；而且经常会有一点……饿肚子。很少有人知道，它们会以这样的速率重新长出那些鞭毛！\n\n呃咳。打扰了。我该如何帮助你？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:232`

### English

Great to see you again! Don't mind those scraping and moaning sounds. We have, uh... Guests, at the moment! They're a bit on the larger side; tend to get a bit... Hungry. And little wonder, at the rate they're regrowing those flagella!\n\nAhem. Excuse me. How can we help you?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:256`

## enclave.305.desc.hostile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.305.desc.hostile&lang=en)

### 中文

嗯嗯嗯，我们之前见过你们这群家伙……\n\n我们跟你们没什么可说的，也没什么服务愿意提供的。麻利地滚一边去吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:233`

### English

Hmm, we've met your ilk before...\n\nWe have nothing to say to you, nor any services to offer. Kindly be on your way.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:257`

## enclave.305.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.305.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们想要§Y拆解一支舰队§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:234`

### English

We'd like to §Yscrap a fleet§!.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:258`

## enclave.305.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.305.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你们现在有什么样的§Y翻新船只§!？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:235`

### English

## enclave.310.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.310.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

太棒了！真的很高兴能能从您的手中接过这份差事。\n\n根据我们的扫描仪，您有下列舰队处于可选范围之内。在附件中可以查看估价，现在请您做出选择吧。\n\n$enclave.310.instructions$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:241`

### English

Splendid! It would be our pleasure to take that off your hands.\n\nAccording to our scanners, you have the following fleets within range. You'll find our estimates attached; the choice is now yours!\n\n$enclave.310.instructions$

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:266`

## enclave.310.organic.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.310.organic.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

棒极了！那将是我们的荣幸……星辰在上，那是什么呀？！你居然称那是一艘舰船？我是说，我能够找到它是怎么……哇哦，它看上去可真是凶巴巴的。拜托了，千万不要冲动行事！我们能够从你那里接手活生生的“舰船”，但恐怕§H我们必须收取清洁费用§!。\n\n根据我们的扫描，你的以下舰船位于范围之内。你能在附件里找到我们的预估；现在由你来做决定了！\n\n$enclave.310.instructions$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:242`

### English

Splendid! It would be our pleasure to... By the stars, what is that?! You call that a vessel? I mean, I can see how it... Wow, it's a mean-looking thing. Please, don't do anything hasty! We can take living 'ships' off your hands too, but I'm afraid §Hwe must charge a clean-up penalty§!.\n\nAccording to our scanners, you have the following fleets within range. You'll find our estimates attached; the choice is now yours!\n\n$enclave.310.instructions$

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:267`

## enclave.310.nofleet.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.310.nofleet.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当然了！我们的服务一向直接明了：开一支舰队到§H[salvager_enclave_system.GetName]§!来，然后和我们打一声招呼。只要我们确认了您是舰队的主人，就能给出估价清单。随后我们就会接管舰队，您也能马上得到回收后的材料。\n\n噢，不过切记：不要把别人的东西带过来拆解了！我们不想因此吃一遍苦头，可不能再翻车了……\n\n$enclave.310.instructions$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:243`

### English

Of course! Our service is straightforward: bring a fleet in here to §H[salvager_enclave_system.GetName]§!, and give us a hail. Once we verify ownership, we can provide you with a list of estimates. We'll then take possession, and your reclaimed materials will be with you in no time.\n\nOh, also: no scrapping of others' merchandise! We do not want to suffer through the kind of conflict that can bring up - not again, at least...\n\n$enclave.310.instructions$

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:268`

## enclave.310.instructions

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.310.instructions&lang=en)

### 中文

§E我们可以将自己的舰船带到打捞者位于§!§H[salvager_enclave_system.GetName]§!§E星系的恒星基地，对自己的舰船进行估价和贱卖。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:244`

### English

§EWe can have our ships appraised and scrapped if we bring them to the Salvagers' starbase in the§! §H[salvager_enclave_system.GetName]§!§E system.§!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:269`

### English

## enclave.320.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.320.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

太神奇了！舰船破破烂烂进来，完完整整出去。目前有以下几种类型可选。\n\n要知道每艘我们翻新的舰船在出售后都享受§H钢铁三包§!。在船坞中翻新后，我们相信这些船只肯定开的比原装出厂时还舒服。\n\n不过请记住：翻新相当耗时。在把一支舰队完整翻新之前，我们是没法卖船给您的。所以您也许需要日后再与我们联系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:253`

### English

Marvelous! When a busted-up ship comes our way, we work hard to restore it back to working life. Right now we have the following types available.\n\nKnow that every vessel we reclaim is sold under our §HDurasteel Guarantee§!. We're confident these ships will run more smoothly now than back when they were shipyard-fresh!\n\nJust bear in mind: reclamation is a time-consuming process. We cannot provide you an entire squadron without having to restock afterwards, so you may need to check back with us in future.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:279`

## enclave.320.timeout.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.320.timeout.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不论什么样的破船进到我们这来，我们都会尽全力使其焕发活力。不过现在§Y我们的码头仓库里似乎空荡荡的§!。\n\n请晚些时候再来，到时候再看看我们有什么能帮您的。每次翻新大概需要花上我们§H2年§!左右。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:254`

### English

Well, whenever a busted-up ship comes our way, we work hard to restore it back to working life. Unfortunately it appears §Your docks are empty§! at the moment!\n\nPlease return later, and we'll see what we can do for you. It usually takes us around... §H2 years§! for a complete set of refurbishments.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:280`

### English

Thank you kindly!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:299`

## enclave.330.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.330.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当然了！只要您腾出给我们工作的地方，我方机械师就能随时关注当地的船只维修状况。\n\n我们会准备好维修工坊模块所需的各种材料，让您可以将其安装到§Y紧邻我方的恒星基地§!上。基地的模块槽位也许需要进行调整，不过一旦我方工程师成功建立工坊，你们就能将船只开进来维修。每隔一段时间，我们还能在贵国疆域内为您进行建造搭把手。\n\n所以，听上去怎么样？这样的§H维修工坊§!还合您的意吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:273`

### English

Certainly! You give us space to work, and our mechanics can fast-track your local ship repairs in no time.\n\nWe'll fix you up with all you need to attach a Salvage Works module onto the §Ystarbase adjoining ours§!. You may need to shift around a bulkhead or two, but once our engineers have a place to set up shop, your ships will be flying right through those repair bays! We can even help out with your construction efforts empire-wide, every once in a while.\n\nSo, how about it: does a §HSalvage Works§! take your fancy?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:301`

## enclave.330.lowopinion.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.330.lowopinion.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当然！我们向最信任的客户们提供一个特殊的机会。简而言之：您给我们在[salvager_enclave_system.GetName]恒星基地工作的空间，我们的机械师就可以紧跟您当地的舰船维修工作。我们甚至可以为您恒星基地和船坞所进行的建设搭把手，全帝国范围内都行！\n\n如果您感兴趣的话，我们很乐意进一步讨论这件事……等到我们的合作关系更加牢固之后。希望您能理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:274`

### English

Certainly! We extend a special opportunity to our most trusted customers. In short: you give us space to work aboard a starbase here in [salvager_enclave_system.GetName], and our mechanics can fast-track your local ship repairs. We can even help out with your starbase and shipyard construction efforts, empire-wide!\n\nIf that's of interest to you, we'll be happy to discuss the matter further... once we feel more sure of this working relationship. We hope you understand.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:302`

## enclave.330.b.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.330.b.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

允许使用§Y$sm_salvager_workshop$§!恒星基地建筑，降低驻港舰队维护费并加快修理速度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:276`

### English

Enables the §Y$sm_salvager_workshop$§! starbase building, reducing upkeep and speeding up repairs for docked ships.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:304`

## enclave.335.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.335.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当然！我们的技术人员一直在努力工作，制定雄心勃勃的新工作计划，我们很荣幸能为您提供限时的生产力增幅。\n\n我们将把您的维修工坊拨到高速档，替您§Y所有恒星基地§!上的工程回收再利用零部件！我们甚至有一些锦囊妙计来帮助您生产舰船。这应该是有用的！大概~\n\n请记住，此类工作是有代价的；我们无法经常提供帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:277`

### English

Of course! Our mechanics have been hard at work devising ambitious new work plans, and it would be our pleasure to extend to you a limited-time frenzy of productivity.\n\nWe'll put that Salvage Works of yours into high gear, reclaiming and repurposing parts for projects on §Yall your starbases§!. We even have a few tricks up our sleeves to help you produce ships, in case that should be useful!\n\nBear in mind that work like this takes its toll; we shan't manage this often.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:306`

## enclave.335.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.335.a&lang=en)

### 中文

$enclave.310.a$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

$enclave.310.a$

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:307`

## enclave.336.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.336.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

再次向你致敬！我很高兴地宣布，我们已经准备好卯足力气将事业提升到一个新的高度，让打捞工作真正务有所值。\n\n你有什么想法？准备好在§Y你所有的恒星基地§!加快舰船建造了吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:279`

### English

Hello again! I'm pleased to say we're ready to kick things back into a higher gear, and get that Salvage Works to really earn its keep.\n\nWhat do you say? Ready to speed up shipbuilding across §Yall your starbases§!?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:309`

## enclave.340.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.340.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

真是相当庄严的召唤啊。听到舰队在战斗中被击沉，我们的痛苦超乎您的想象，但太空中的生活是残酷的，有些事情不可避免。\n\n……请原谅我的遐思。毫无疑问，那是我们能提供的服务。\n\n$enclave.340.main.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:280`

### English

A most solemn calling. It pains us more deeply than you can know to hear of ships stricken in battle, but life is harsh out here in the vacuum, and needs must.\n\n... Please, forgive my reverie. Absolutely, that is a service we can provide.\n\n$enclave.340.main.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:311`

## enclave.340.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.340.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

唉。所以，严酷的真空已经吞噬了更多船只……当然，我们很乐意从您所说的悲惨战场上回收一些积极的东西。\n\n$enclave.340.main.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:281`

### English

Alas. So, the harsh vacuum has claimed more ships... But of course, we will gladly attempt to reclaim something positive from this tragic battle you speak of.\n\n$enclave.340.main.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:312`

## enclave.340.main.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.340.main.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

如果您愿意授予我们§Y打捞权§!并为我们提供残骸场的§Y坐标§!，我们将会在合适的时候回报您。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:282`

### English

If you would be so kind as to grants us §Ysalvage rights§! and furnish us with §Yco-ordinates§! for these debris fields, we shall compensate you in due course.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:313`

## enclave.340.nodebris.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.340.nodebris.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

啊，是的，战斗：这是打捞的最悲哀的原因……您得原谅我，[root.GetRulerTitle]；野蛮的毁灭不会给我们带来欢乐。然而，我们明白这是不可避免的。如果你们的船不愿或不能筛查残骸，我们的确可以承接打捞工作。\n\n有需要的时候请来找我们，并赋予我们打捞的权利。我们会用在那里发现的任何材料和知识来补偿您。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:283`

### English

Ah yes, battle: that most sorrowful cause of salvage...\n\nYou'll have to forgive me, [root.GetRulerTitle]; barbaric destruction brings us no joy. However, we understand that needs must. We can indeed handle salvage under commission, if your ships are unwilling or unable to sift through the debris.\n\nCome to us when need arises, and grant us the rights of salvage. We will then undertake to compensate you for whatever materials and knowledge we find out there.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:314`

## enclave.340.lowopinion.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.340.lowopinion.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我为打捞工作最悲哀的原因而哀叹：战斗。请原谅我，[root.GetRulerTitle]，我们有理由对这样的破坏表示哀悼。不过，我们确实可以承接打捞工作。但这是我们向§Y更为熟络的客户§!提供的服务。\n\n有需要的时候请来找我们，我们将处理属于你们的§H残骸场§!。我们会用在那里发现的任何材料和工程知识来补偿您。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:284`

### English

Alas for that most sorrowful cause of salvage: battle.\n\nYou'll have to forgive me, [root.GetRulerTitle]; we have our reasons for mourning such willful destruction. However, for our §Yregular customers§! we can indeed handle salvage under commission.\n\nReturn to us later, and when the need arises. We will undertake to deal with §Hdebris fields§! you have claim to, and shall compensate you for whatever materials and engineering knowledge we find out there.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:315`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.340.a&lang=en)

### English

## enclave.340.b.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.340.b.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将§R无法进入§!至多§Y3处残骸场§!。[salvager_enclave_country.GetName]将§Y在90天内§!将回收的技术或材料交付给我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:286`

### English

We shall §Rlose access§! to up to §Y3 debris fields§!. The [salvager_enclave_country.GetName] will deliver salvaged technology or materials to us §Ywithin 90 days§!.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:317`

## enclave.341.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.341.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

谢谢您！打捞权看来在按部就班地转让；回收工作进展顺利。我们将很快联系您。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:287`

### English

Thank you. The transfer of salvage rights looks to be in order; the claim is good. We'll be in touch soon.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:319`

## enclave.342.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.342.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

噢！我很抱歉这么说，你的这堆破烂玩意好像在我们的谈话期间已经劣化的不像样子了。即使是我们也缺乏足够的技能将已经坏了这么久的残骸复原。啊呀，这是我们的老对手，熵，在趁势反击呢！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:288`

### English

Oh! I'm sorry to say, it looks as though those debris fields of yours have deteriorated over the course of our conversation. Even we lack the skills to recover a wreck which has been left that long. Alas, our old foe entropy strikes again!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:320`

## enclave.345.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.345.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这将是我们的荣幸！清闲的[salvager_enclave_country.species.GetHandNamePlural]头脑灵活，这对我们来说意义非凡！我们将非常乐意伸出援手并与我们的§Y老顾客§!分享一些实用的技巧，毕竟我们的生意可是受您照顾有加。\n\n我们也会感谢您用于支持我们简陋的恒星基地的任何捐赠！老旧的船体可以多次改造，不过有时候，崭新熔炼的舱壁的可靠性无可匹敌。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:289`

### English

It would be our pleasure! Idle [salvager_enclave_country.species.GetHandNamePlural] make curious minds, and that counts double for us! We are most happy to lend aid and share handy tips with our §Yregular customers§! - so long as our modest expenses are covered.\n\nWe would also appreciate any donation you can make to help shore up our humble starbase! Old hulls can be repurposed many times over, but sometimes there's just no beating the reliability of a freshly-smelted bulkhead.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:322`

## enclave.345.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.345.a&lang=en)

### 中文

$enclave.310.a$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:290`

### English

$enclave.310.a$

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:323`

## enclave.346.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.346.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你好，朋友！我们多年以来的合作研究一直进行得不错。我听说的是，我们的几位工程师采纳了一些来自你方的建议，同时你也采纳了一些我们得建议！\n\n然而，当初达成的合约时间已经到期了，我们也希望让这些思维活跃的学徒们重新回到我们家里面做些事情……也就是说，除非你愿意再多给我们一点补偿，让他们再留一阵子？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:291`

### English

Hello, friends! Our years of collaborative study have really whisked by. The way I hear it, a few of our engineers have picked up a few tips from you, as well as you from us!\n\nHowever, the agreed upon time has now elapsed and we could use those keen-minded apprentices of ours back at home... That is, unless you're willing to compensate us for a while longer?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:325`

## enclave.350.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.350.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

作为我们§Y长期客源§!的一员，我们自然很乐意派遣一名专家来督导你的打捞工作，只需要付很少的价钱。一开工就能拖到一大串新近烧焦的残骸，这个作为欢迎仪式想必很合适。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:292`

### English

As one of our more §Yregular customers§!, we would certainly be willing to send someone to oversee your scrapping efforts, for a small cut. It will be a welcome diversion to trawl freshly-charred wrecks.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:327`

## enclave.350.desc.not_available

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.350.desc.not_available&lang=en)

### 中文

我们为自己的§Y长期客源§!提供服务，会派遣一名督工协助改进你的打捞工作，只需要付很少的价钱。不幸的是，我们现在没有可供派遣的督工——他们现在简直是供不应求，恐怕我们一些其他的客户享有优先权。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:293`

### English

We offer our §Yregular customers§! the services of an overseer who can improve your scrapping efforts, for a small cut. Unfortunately, none of our overseers are available at the moment - they are in high demand, and I'm afraid some of our other customers take priority.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:328`

## enclave.350.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.350.a&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:294`

### English
