Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:329`

## enclave.352.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.352.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

致敬！我们向你方派去的督工已经回到我们这里，其服务任期已经完成。然而，如果你仍然需要我们的服务，我们很乐意再派遣一名替补。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:295`

### English

Greetings! The overseer we had sent to you has returned to us, their term in the field complete. However, we would certainly be willing to send a replacement, if you still have use for our services.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:330`

## master_salvager

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amaster_salvager&lang=en)

### 中文

打捞大师

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:296`

### English

Master Salvager

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:332`

## master_salvager_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amaster_salvager_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一名来自打捞者城邦的经验丰富的技师，负责确保战场残骸被最充分地利用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:297`

### English

An experienced technician from the Salvager Enclave ensures battlefield debris is put to efficient use.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:333`

## master_salvager_catch_phrase

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amaster_salvager_catch_phrase&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“好极了，让我们做一些野外工作吧！有什么有趣的残骸漂浮着吗？”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:298`

### English

§L"Right, let's do some field work! Got any interesting wreckage floating about?"§!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:335`

## leader_trait_master_salvager

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_master_salvager&lang=en)

$master_salvager$

### English

$master_salvager$

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:337`

## leader_trait_master_salvager_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_master_salvager_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一位来自[salvager_enclave_country.GetName]的老练打捞者，以低廉的价格共享着他们的技术。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:300`

### English

A veteran of Salvage operations from the [salvager_enclave_country.GetName], sharing their expertise for a small fee.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:338`

## leader_trait_master_salvager_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_master_salvager_effect&lang=en)

### 中文

$SYSTEM_EFFECTS_TXT$：\n$t$星系中的友方舰船：\n$tt$每月船体值维修：§G+2%§!\n$t$当星系中有残骸被打捞或研究完成：\n$tt$打捞舰船的概率：§Y15%§!\n$tt$$salvage_ship_restrictions$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:301`

### English

$SYSTEM_EFFECTS_TXT$:\n$t$Friendly Ships in System:\n$tt$Monthly Hull Points Repaired: §G+2%§!\n$t$When Debris is Scavenged/Researched in System:\n$tt$Chance to Salvage Ships: §Y15%§!\n$tt$$salvage_ship_restrictions$

## enclave.360.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.360.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

一列发自§Y[salvager_enclave_country.GetName]§!的货运驳船刚刚到达，装载着一批回收的资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:311`

### English

A train of cargo barges has just arrived from the §Y[salvager_enclave_country.GetName]§!, carrying a consignment of reclaimed resources.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:353`

## enclave.360.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.360.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$enclave.360.intro$\n\n打捞人员随货附上了一封感谢信，以及一份有形的礼物——某种动态艺术品。它在我们人工重力场中运动的方式令人不安，但也相当迷人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:312`

### English

$enclave.360.intro$\n\nThe Salvagers enclosed a message expressing their thanks, as well as a tangible gift - some form of kinetic artwork. The way it moves within our artificial gravity fields is unnerving, and yet quite captivating.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:354`

## enclave.360.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.360.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$enclave.360.intro$\n\n货运员代表打捞者们不住地表示着感谢，直到他在我们货运拖船的发动机遮罩前怔住。很遗憾，在卸货之前，我们必须强行赶走这位激情满满、充斥着好奇心的狂热人士。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:313`

### English

$enclave.360.intro$\n\nThe courier was relentless in expressing thanks on behalf of the Salvagers - that is, until they became transfixed by the engine housings on our own cargo tugs. This impassioned and deeply curious enthusiast had to be forcibly removed before any unloading of cargo could commence.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:355`

## enclave.360.c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.360.c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$enclave.360.intro$\n\n被货物围绕在中间的是一个§H蓝图§!的数据库，尽管画得很急，但是每一个细节都很到位。看上去这份作品的作者，要么对于自己的发现十分兴奋，要么就是在研究某种教育形式的图表。\n\n不管是哪一种，[salvager_enclave_country.GetName]已经将此人的研究呈递给我们使用，并送上了自己的赞美。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:314`

### English

$enclave.360.intro$\n\nEnclosed amongst the cargo is a database of §Hblueprints§!, hastily-drawn yet quite thorough in their detail. It appears to be the work of someone who was either very excited by their find, or else was studying the schematics as some form of education.\n\nEither way, the [salvager_enclave_country.GetName] has presented their studies for our use, with their compliments.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:356`

## enclave.360.d.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.360.d.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$enclave.360.intro$\n\n货运员看上去很紧张；看得出来此人巴不得去跑其他的差事。这个鬼灵精怪的[salvager_enclave_country.GetSpeciesName]看上去急着要把给我们的食物供给品一股脑弄走。确实，这些送到手上的食物包装非常精美——几乎就像是在某些地方制造出来的替代品一样。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:315`

### English

$enclave.360.intro$\n\nThe courier appeared quite nervous; visibly keen to resume some other errand. This skittish [salvager_enclave_country.GetSpeciesName] seemed in a particular hurry to divest the food supplies we have been owed. Indeed, these morsels come to us surprisingly well-packaged - almost as though a substitution has been made somewhere...

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:357`

## enclave.360.e.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.360.e.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$enclave.360.intro$\n\n货运员看上去很紧张；看得出来此人巴不得去跑其他的差事。这个鬼灵精怪的[salvager_enclave_country.GetSpeciesName]很谦恭地将那些本次打捞路途上收获的各类§H蓝图§!交到我们手上。然而，在运送食物供给品的时候，场面出现了明显的匆忙。\n\n确实，尽管未经证实，有人看见类似长相的太空生物最近出现在了[salvager_enclave_system.GetName]星系内……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:316`

### English

$enclave.360.intro$\n\nThe courier appeared quite nervous; visibly keen to resume some other errand. This skittish [salvager_enclave_country.GetSpeciesName] was courteous enough in handing over various §Hblueprints§! they say came their way during the course of this salvage. There was, however, some apparent urgency behind their delivery of food supplies.\n\nIndeed, there have been unconfirmed sightings of familiar-looking, spaceborne creatures in the [salvager_enclave_system.GetName] system of late...

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:358`

## enclave.365.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.365.name&lang=en)

### 中文

委托打捞

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:317`

### English

Commissioned Salvage

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:360`

## enclave.365.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.365.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一艘发自从§Y[salvager_enclave_country.GetName]§!的快递驳船已经满载各种蓝图和一小批资源抵达！所有这些都是从残骸中打捞回收的。\n\n这艘驳船的领航员除了对我们的惠顾表示礼貌的感谢外很少说话。交接现场的气氛十分严肃。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:318`

### English

A courier barge has arrived from the §Y[salvager_enclave_country.GetName]§!, carrying various blueprints and a small consignment of resources. All were reclaimed from debris salvage.\n\nThe pilot of this barge spoke little, other than to express polite thanks for our custom. The handover was treated with a great deal of solemnity.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:361`

## enclave.375.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.375.name&lang=en)

### 中文

打捞者城邦被毁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:323`

### English

Salvager Enclave Destroyed

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:367`

## enclave.375.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.375.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[salvager_system.GetName]恒星系的进攻是一次反响斐然的成功。[FROM.GetName]被摧毁，所剩无几的幸存者逃向了深空。由于他们的设施被毁于一旦，他们那可怜巴巴的服务也成为了过去式。星系里再也没有这帮子人的踪影了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:324`

### English

Our offensive in the [salvager_system.GetName] system was a resounding success. The [FROM.GetName] is destroyed, and the few remaining survivors have scattered to the void. With their facilities in ruin, their petty services are a thing of the past. The galaxy is well rid of them.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:368`

## enclave.380.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.380.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到报告，[salvager_system.GetName]恒星系的[FROM.GetName]太空城邦被毁。袭击者来去无影，一瞬间就把打捞者的设施变成了一堆废墟。所剩无几的幸存者四散逃离，看来，现在整个星系要学会习惯于没有他们提供服务的新生活了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

We have received reports that the [FROM.GetName] Enclave in the [salvager_system.GetName] system has been destroyed. The aggressors made short work of the campaign, reducing the Salvagers' facilities to rubble. The few remaining survivors have scattered, and it appears that for the time being the galaxy will have to make do without their services.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:369`

## enclave.405.generic.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.405.generic.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

啊哈！一位来访者。欢迎欢迎。\n\n我们是§H[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]§!。在这个自虚空诞生的舱室里，我们的女巫集会谈论着凡人所费解的力量。我们思索着潜藏梦境中的秘密、探寻着原始灵能的贮藏地、凝视着驱动整个文明的无形齿轮……§Y虚境§!对于聆听者，揭示良多。\n\n此时此刻，我聆听于你。什么风把你吹来了？你已知否？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:342`

### English

Ah! A visitor. Welcome.\n\nWe are the §H[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]§!. Within this void-borne hull, our coven communes with forces incomprehensible to most mortal minds. We contemplate the secrets that lie hidden in dreams, reservoirs of untapped potential, the unseen gears driving whole civilizations... §YThe Shroud§! reveals much to those who would listen.\n\nAt this moment, I listen to you. What brings you to us? Do you yet know?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:389`

## enclave.405.generic.n.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.405.generic.n.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

欢迎回来，来访者。我们等候多时。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:343`

### English

Welcome back, visitor. We have been waiting.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:390`

## enclave.405.generic.o.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.405.generic.o.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

许多文明都讲述着同样的故事，在过于幽深的矿洞里潜伏着黑暗的存在。那么来访者，请问你是否好奇过，在我们层层的现实之下隐藏着什么?

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:344`

### English

So many civilizations tell the same kind of story, of dark things lurking in mines dug too deep. Tell me, visitor: have you ever wondered what lurks beneath the stratified layers of our reality?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:391`

## enclave.405.generic.p.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.405.generic.p.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们都知道一句谚语:\n“被风吹进来的东西终将被雨冲出去。”\n对于许多居住在短命行星上的居民来说，算是慰藉之辞……\n\n在被我们称为虚境的世界里，可没有这样的天气。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:345`

### English

There is a saying known to us:\n"What is blown in by the wind will be washed out by the rain."\nComforting words for many a short-lived planet-dweller...\n\nThe realm we call the Shroud knows no such weather.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:392`

## enclave.405.psionic.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.405.psionic.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

啊哈，是不可知世界的旅伴！我们§H[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]§!欢迎你并视你为朋友。\n\n请原谅这种低劣的沟通方式。我们所践行的强烈的灵能祷告，恐怕只能基于可畏的心灵感应屏障和通路而施行。因此，我们只能从物质世界中接收思想之一瞥。\n\n所以我不禁要问：是什么风把你吹来的?

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:346`

### English

Ah, fellow sojourners of the unknowable! We, the §H[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]§! welcome you and name you friends.\n\nPlease forgive this inferior means of communication. The intense, psionic devotions we practise may only be conducted from behind fearsome telepathic barriers and conduits. Thus, we may only receive glimpses of thought from the corporeal realm.\n\nSo it is that I must ask: what brings you to us?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:393`

## enclave.405.psionic.n.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.405.psionic.n.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

那么，我们的旅伴回来了！很高兴再次看到你。我们等候多时。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:347`

### English

So, the sojourners return! It is pleasant to see you again. We have been waiting.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:394`

## enclave.405.psionic.o.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.405.psionic.o.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们§H[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]§!坚持的所有信条中，少有比这一条更坚定的：即使地位崇高如我们一般，也肯定能在星海中找到同伴。\n\n那么，我飞升了的朋友：我们能为彼此做些什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:348`

### English

Of all the tenets we hold to in the §H[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]§!, few are held more dearly than this: even those as elevated as ourselves can be sure of finding our fellows in the galaxy.\n\nSo, fellow ascendent one: how may we serve each other?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:395`

## enclave.405.hostile.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.405.hostile.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

闯入者，你的敌意就像恶臭的瘴气一样笼罩着自己。我们是和平的集会，但你要知道：如果你想让我们遭受苦难，你会发现这种愚蠢的行径将以十倍奉还。我们是§H[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]§!，尽管我们看似鞭长莫及，但我们的愤怒将会毁天灭地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:349`

### English

Interloper, your hostility precedes you like a foul miasma. We are a peaceful coven, but know this: if you intend to inflict harm upon us, you shall find such folly visited back upon you tenfold. We are the §H[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]§!, and though our reach may appear lacking, our wrath is devastating.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:396`

## enclave.405.desc.mindwardens

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.405.desc.mindwardens&lang=en)

### 中文

你可真是名过其实。\n\n我们拒绝与畏惧§H虚境§!之人共商大计。你们对宇宙的狭隘认知终将令我们所有人固步自封。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:350`

### English

Your reputation precedes you.\n\nWe shall hold no counsel with those who fear the §HShroud§!. Your limited vision of the cosmos will chain us all to the ground.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:397`

## enclave.405.desc.mindwardens_trust

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.405.desc.mindwardens_trust&lang=en)

### 中文

[mindwarden_enclave_country.GetName]的无知偏见毒害了你的思想。多么讽刺啊……\n\n我们绝不会与和那些蠢货有染的人共商前途。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:351`

### English

The ignorant prejudices of the [mindwarden_enclave_country.GetName] have poisoned your mind. What irony...\n\nWe will hold no counsel with those who hold such ties with fools.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:398`

## enclave.405.desc.end_of_the_cycle

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.405.desc.end_of_the_cycle&lang=en)

### 中文

“你唤醒了远超你理解的恐怖，它宣告了星系的末日。\n\n我们不愿去面对这样的疯狂事物。离我们远点！”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:352`

### English

"You have awoken horror far beyond your understanding, and with it have doomed this galaxy.\n\nWe are unwilling to court madness itself. Be gone from us!"

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:399`

### English

### English

We seek mastery of the §YShroud§!.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:414`

## enclave.410.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.410.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

啊哈，一个重要的问题——和前人所问相比，深刻程度可谓不相上下。这么一个问题有不菲的价格，不过我们可以回答。\n\n然而，要知道：虚境允许我们感知尚未发生的事件，而这些知识将帮助指导你们的道路。但任何单一的事件都不足以撼动星系的运转。\n\n如果你有意达成这笔交易，那么相比于拓展抱负，你应该去做更充分的准备。但是，哦，哪怕是对顷刻将至之事的最细微一瞥，都可能建立起什么样的宏伟计划啊……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

### English

Ah, a momentous query - no less poignant for being asked by so many before you. Such a question carries a heavy price, but we can provide answers.\n\nKnow this, however: the Shroud allows us to perceive events which have not yet come to pass, and this knowledge shall help guide your course. But it is beyond the power of any one event to alter the turning of the galaxy.\n\nIf you would strike this bargain, know that your aspirations should extend no further than to be better prepared. But oh, what grand designs may be built upon even the merest glimpse at what is to come...

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:416`

## enclave.410.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.410.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们还是考虑别的事情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

We will consider something else.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:417`

## enclave.410.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.410.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将支付代价。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:370`

### English

We shall pay.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:418`

## enclave.415.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.415.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境在战栗。它在那一刻似乎一言不发，但又诉说了一切。时间的无限之织缕从此时此刻延伸开来，进入了汇合之流。此处，这个地方，前方的道路逐渐明了。预知仪式已经开始了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

The Shroud shivers. It speaks without voice and in all voices at once. The infinite strands of time that extend from this moment now enter into confluence. Here, in this place, the path ahead grows clear. The rite of divination has begun.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:419`

## enclave.415.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.415.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]§!将离开几日以便与虚境长谈。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:372`

### English

The §Y[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]§! will retire for a few days in order to commune with the Shroud.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:420`

## enclave.416.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.416.name&lang=en)

### 中文

迹象消逝

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:374`

### English

The Sign Fades

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:423`

## enclave.416.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.416.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“虚境变得安静了下来。再一次，疲倦的时间之河退回到了它陈腐的航道中。预知仪式昭告的改变已经成为了过去。现在带着敬畏离开，因为你已经映射了虚境的造业。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:375`

### English

"The Shroud grows quiet. Once more, the weary river of time recedes into its well-worn course. The changes foretold by the rite of divination have come to pass. Go now in awe as you reflect on the workings of the Shroud."

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:424`

## enclave.420.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.420.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

星系十分黑暗，危险无处不在。为了点亮朝圣者的路途，我们提供§H虚境信标§!。一旦在你的某个恒星基地上面安装这个，它的光芒可以穿透一切；哪怕是某些人称之为现实的缥缈薄暮。它延伸出来的网道可以穿过虚境，为凡间的舰船跨越巨大的时空前往遥远的距离提供了一种手段。\n\n要谨慎地选择你的信标的位置——虚境隧道一旦生成，就不能被移除，也没有可能再召唤出另一个信标。\n\n你要将以下的话语牢记在心：虚境隧道不是普通的道路，就算是我们也无法预测使用这种手段会有什么后果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:376`

### English

The galaxy is dark, and full of dangers. To illumine the path of wayward pilgrims we offer the §HShroud Beacon§!. Once installed on one of your starbases, its light pierces all; even that thin veil some call reality. The Tunnel it spawns offers a bypass through the Shroud, and a means for corporeal vessels to traverse vast distances through space and time.\n\nChoose the location of your Beacon carefully - once established, a Shroud Tunnel cannot be moved, and no secondary Beacon can call another forth.\n\nYou would do well to heed these words: a Shroud Tunnel is no ordinary road, and not even we can foretell all the consequences of such a journey.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:426`

我们想要一个§Y虚境信标§!。

### English

## shroud_beacon_info

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashroud_beacon_info&lang=en)

### 中文

允许在己方某个恒星基地建造一个§H虚境信标§!。一旦完工，一个§H虚境隧道§!会在恒星系中生成，并提供一个通往§Y[shroudwalker_enclave_system.GetName]恒星系§!的双向隧道。我们只能生成一个虚境隧道；因此请谨慎选择恒星系的位置。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:379`

### English

Permits building a §HShroud Beacon§! on one of our Starbases. Once built, it will spawn a §HShroud Tunnel§!, providing passage to and from the §Y[shroudwalker_enclave_system.GetName] system§!. Only one Tunnel can be spawned - choose its location carefully.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:429`

## enclave.425.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.425.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一阵低语传达至我们的意识：虚境的道路缥缈不定，变换万千。愿这个信标点亮你前行的道路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:380`

### English

A saying is known to us: the paths of the Shroud are strange and winding. May this Beacon light your way forward.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:430`

## enclave.435.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.435.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境行者城邦被毁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:381`

### English

Shroudwalker Enclave Destroyed

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:432`

## enclave.435.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.435.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[shroudwalker_system.GetName]恒星系的大胜利！我们的军队快刀斩乱麻地解决了那些所谓的[FROM.GetName]秘术师。尽管成天对知识和力量夸夸其谈，他们对虚境的愚蠢奉献还是没能预先料知到我们的攻击。事实上，我们的军队很轻易地就破坏了设施的机械化防御。然而，在进入之后，他们发现整个太空城邦人去楼空，只有一个孤独的侍僧在一个偏僻的房室里面进行冥想。剩下的人去了哪里，我们无从得知，但是整个星系这下摆脱了这种空洞的教条。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:382`

### English

Victory in the [shroudwalker_system.GetName] system! Our forces made short work of the so-called mystics of the [FROM.GetName]. For all the grand talk of knowledge and power, their foolish devotion to the Shroud could not provide clairvoyance enough to forestall our assault. In fact, our forces easily breached the facility's mechanical defenses. However, once inside, they discovered the enclave empty, save for a lone acolyte they found deep in meditation in a remote chamber. Where the rest have gone, we have no idea, but the galaxy is well rid of such hollow dogma.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:433`

## enclave.440.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.440.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

传达的报告主要围绕着[shroudwalker_system.GetName]恒星系发生的[FROM.GetName]的毁灭。侵略者在这次行动中十分迅速，将虚境行者的设施化为一片断壁残垣。很难相信这些秘术大师没有预料到自身的消亡。还是说，这场悲剧仅仅只是某个更庞大计划的一部分？谁又能说得准呢？不过，没有他们的效劳，未来仍然充满重重迷雾。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:383`

### English

Reports are coming in surrounding the destruction of the [FROM.GetName] in the [shroudwalker_system.GetName] system. The aggressors made short work of the campaign, reducing the Shroudwalkers' facilities to rubble. It is strange to think that these mystics could not foresee their own demise. Or could this tragedy be a part of some still grander design? Who can say? Without their services, the future remains shrouded.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:434`

## divination_visitors_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adivination_visitors_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是虚境的意志，你们的世界将被拜访。这些外来者是否会带来坏消息仍是个有待探索的奥秘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:402`

### English

It is the will of the Shroud that your worlds be visited. Whether these strangers bring ill tidings or not is a mystery yet to be experienced.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:458`

## divination_locus_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adivination_locus_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是虚境的意志，你周围的空间以新的形式呈现。谨慎但大胆地行走吧，要考虑前方的道路上可能会有什么奇迹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:408`

### English

It is the will of the Shroud that the space around you take on new forms. Tread carefully but boldly, and consider what marvels may lie on the paths ahead.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:465`

### English

We embrace the Shroud's will by exploring these paths ourselves

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:466`

## enclave.4200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.4200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在黑暗中挣扎了很长一段时间之后，虚境的面纱被揭开了。虽然我们的科学家对虚境信标运作的奇异过程仍然知之甚少，但它的物理系统似乎与灵能纠缠在一起，它的运作几乎像一个活的有机体。它生成的隧道与其说是路线，不如说是近道，为我们的舰船提供了一条通过虚境的超维度捷径。星际探索如今将变得更快……而且更神秘得多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:415`

### English

After a long period spent grasping in the dark, the veil that covers the Shroud has been lifted. Although the strange processes by which the Shroud Beacon functions remain little understood by our scientists, its physical systems appear entangled with psionic energy, its operations almost like those of a living organism. The Tunnel it spawns is more bypass than route, offering our vessels an extradimensional shortcut via the Shroud. Interstellar exploration will now be quicker... and infinitely more mysterious.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:475`

## enclave.4240.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.4240.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最终觉醒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:417`

### English

Final Awakening

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:479`

## enclave.4240.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.4240.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

学徒们，虚境宣昭了警告。巨大的危机近在眼前，整个星系都处在毁灭的边缘。我们的预知显示，我们自身的力量无法匹配即将到来的威胁。与其被毁灭，我们现在将灌输给你们最后一课：所有的幻梦都有苏醒之时。我们离开这个世界的时刻已经到了。尽管虚境十分黑暗，飘忽不定，我们最终将会升往一个新的位面，一个新的世界，在那里继续传播我们的教义。我们将离开这个星系，将它的命运交付给你。希望我们以往的功课能在必要之时为你提供指引。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:418`

### English

Apprentices, the Shroud has given warning. A moment of great crisis is at hand, and the galaxy stands on the brink of ruin. Our divinations have shown we are no match for the coming threat. Rather than be destroyed, we now impart our final lesson: every dream has its awakening. The time has come for us to pass beyond this world. Though the paths of the Shroud are dark and winding, we will come at last to a new plane, a new world in which to spread our teachings. We leave this galaxy and its fate to you. May our past lessons be a guide in your time of need.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:480`

## enclave.4243.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.4243.name&lang=en)

### 中文

一份警告

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:425`

### English

A Warning

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:489`

## enclave.4243.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.4243.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

学徒们，你正在走上一条危险的道路。你以为养育了你们数个世纪的我们会没有注意到吗？我们教导你虚境，是为了启蒙，为了提升。但是这个？力量、暴虐、以及你们亲手酿造的危机。牢记我的教诲！这个宇宙不过是一场幻梦的绰影。为时未晚，赶在你被它毁掉之前，马上放弃这误入歧途的野心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:426`

### English

Apprentices, you walk a dangerous path. Did you think we would not notice, we who have nurtured you for centuries? In teaching you of the Shroud, we hoped to enlighten, to elevate. But this? Power and violence, and a crisis of your own making. Remember your teachings! This universe is nothing but the shadow of a dream. There is still time. Give up this misguided ambition before you are destroyed by it.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:490`

## enclave.4243.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.4243.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们变成了什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:427`

### English

What have we become?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:491`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.4243.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的命运十分清晰。不要挡我们的道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:428`

### English

Our destiny is clear. Do not stand in our way.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:492`

## enclave.4245.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.4245.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教育的失败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:429`

### English

A Failure of Education

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:494`

## enclave.4245.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.4245.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境自身在战栗，一切迹象都指向毁灭。常年以来我们注视着你，但即便是我们也无法预见你竟愚蠢到如此境地。欲速则不达。心急地攫取力量，那么你注定会失去它。你忘记了最基本的一课：虚境不是能用来开发的资源，而是用来品鉴的谜题。但是学生的覆辙和老师没有关系。相比于目睹你会成为怎样的可憎之物，我们宁愿离开这个世界。尽管虚境的道路黑暗且变幻不定，我们最终会到达一个新的位面，新的世界。或许在那里我们会找到有价值的学生，传达我们的教义。\n\n希望虚境能对你展现仁慈。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:430`

### English

The Shroud itself shivers, and all signs point to ruin. Long have we watched you, but even we could not have foreseen the extent of your folly. Rushing to act, you will falter. In grasping power, it has already been lost. You have fogotten the most basic lesson: the Shroud is not a resource to exploit, but a mystery to be savoured. But the failure of the student rests with the teacher. Rather than witness the horror you will become, we have chosen to depart this world. Though the paths of the Shroud are dark and winding, we will come at last to a new plane, a new world. Perhaps there, we will find apprentices worthy of our teachings.\n\nMay the Shroud have mercy on you.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:495`

## enclave.4245.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.4245.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你离开得很明智。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:431`

### English

You are wise to leave.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:496`

## enclave.4250.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.4250.name&lang=en)

### 中文

灵能裂口

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:434`

### English

Psionic Breach

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:500`

## enclave.4250.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.4250.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们最近在虚境隧道穿越的时候在时空中造成了一个巨大的裂口。前所未有的能级的灵能能量从恒星系中喷涌而出……然后汇聚在一起。传感器数据在涨落，激变，但是它看上去正逐渐成为某种虚境生物。庞大，而且肯定富有侵略性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:435`

### English

Our recent trip through the Shroud Tunnel has precipitated a massive breach in spacetime. Psionic energy at unprecedented levels is spilling out into the system and... It has begun to coalesce. Sensor data is fluctuating, erratic, but it appears to be some form of Shroud spawn. Large, and definitely aggressive.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:501`

## enclave.4250.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.4250.a&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:436`

### English

The galaxy grows ever more dangerous.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:502`

## enclave.5055.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5055.name&lang=en)

### 中文

迷失虚境

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:441`

### English

Shrouded

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:508`

## enclave.5055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]所预示的变化降临了：在一次意想不到的灵能波动之后，[Root.GetName]在虚境中迷失了。时空本身如荡漾着的巨浪一般涌起，卷起那艘舰船。几秒钟后，它继续发送遥测数据。数据中包括一段失真的船员录像，他们的身体时时刻刻不断变化，扭曲成奇怪的新形状。\n\n从那以后，这艘船的残像或“幽灵”在整个星域乍隐乍现。研究这一现象可以让我们更好地理解这艘船和船员的命运。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:442`

### English

The change foretold by the [shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName] has arrived: after an unexpected spike in psionic fluctuations, the [Root.GetName] was lost to the Shroud. Rising up like a great, rippling wave, spacetime itself curled over the ship. For several seconds afterwards, it continued to transmit telemetry. This included a garbled recording of the crew, their bodies shifting from moment to moment, distorting into strange new forms.\n\nSince then, an afterimage or 'ghost' of the vessel has fleetingly appeared throughout the sector. Studying the phenomenon should allow us to better understand the fate of the ship and its crew.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:509`

## SHROUDED_SHIP_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHROUDED_SHIP_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在虚境里迷失的科研船的波动残像，零星地出现在整个恒星系中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:447`

### English

A fluctuating afterimage of the science ship lost to the Shroud appears sporadically throughout the system.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:515`

## enclave.5060.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5060.name&lang=en)

### 中文

概念之船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:448`

### English

Ship as Concept

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:517`

## enclave.5060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们有在虚境迷失的科研船的最新消息。在整个恒星系中出现的残像并不是飞船本身。相反，我们的科学家将其描述为一个“概念性物体”。这艘“概念之船”以灵能蓝图的形式存在，没有维度。根据观察者的心理状态，船的形式会断断续续、变化无常。研究这一现象有助于我们更好地理解感知与时空之间的联系，但我们并没有离找回最初的飞船更进一步。目前来说，我们只能认为它在虚境里迷失了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:449`

### English

We have an update on the science vessel lost to the Shroud. The afterimage that has been appearing throughout the system is not the ship itself. Instead, our scientists describe it as a 'conceptual object.' Possessing no dimensions, this 'ship as concept' exists as a psionic blueprint. Depending on the mental state of the observer, the ship's form will stutter and change. While studying the phenomenon has helped us to better understand the connections between perception and spacetime, we are no closer to retrieving the original ship. For now, we must consider it lost to the Shroud.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:518`

## enclave.5065.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5065.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最终形式

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:450`

### English

Final Form

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:520`

## enclave.5065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们那艘一度被认为永远迷失在虚境之中的失踪科研船突然又出现了。虽然船的尺寸发生了微妙的改变，船体本身和其中的船员仍保有良好的状态。\n\n船员们把在虚境里的经历描述成类似一场光怪陆离、支离破碎的梦境的东西。随着时间的推移，他们慢慢发现自己可以与周围的环境共筑成一种“共同概念”。随着他们的自我感知逐渐重整，这些船员们一点一点地重回到我们的现实世界。就实际效果而言，他们成功梦到自己重新存在了。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:451`

### English

Our missing science vessel, once thought lost to the Shroud forever, has suddenly reappeared. Although the dimensions of the ship have been been subtly altered, the vessel and its crew are in good shape.\n\nDescribing their experience in the Shroud as something akin to a strange, fragmentary dream, over time the crew was able to forge a 'shared concept' of themselves and their environment. As their individual perceptions began to align, the crew inched ever closer to our reality. In effect, they have succeeded in dreaming themselves back into existence. 

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:521`

## enclave.5065.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5065.a&lang=en)

### 中文

欢迎回来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:452`

### English

Welcome back.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:522`

## enclave.5005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

过度清醒

## enclave.5005.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5005.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]所预言的事件似乎降临了。一种新的街头毒品在[Root.GetName]越来越受欢迎。这种灵能化合物被称为“清醒”，来源于当地一种类似的植物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:463`

### English

It appears the event foretold by the [shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName] has arrived: a new street drug is gaining popularity on [Root.GetName]. Dubbed "Lucid", the psionic compound is derived from a local plant analog.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:534`

## enclave.5005.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5005.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

更重要的是，一些使用者报告说，他们在梦中被观察着，就好像有人或什么东西“越过边界”进入了他们的大脑。然而，许多人认为，与殖民生活的严酷现实相比，梦中的欢欣感更令人沉醉。不幸的是，毒品的流行导致了犯罪分销网络的兴起。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:464`

### English

What's more, several users have reported being watched in their dreams, as if someone - or something - had "crossed a border" into their minds. Nevertheless, many regard the euphoria of the dream state as preferable to the harsh realities of colonial life. Unfortunately, the drug's popularity has led to the rise of a criminal distribution network.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:535`

### English

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

纷乱梦境

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:466`

### English

Troublous Dreams

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:538`

## enclave.5010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

药物“清醒”的使用在[Root.GetName]上继续上升。殖民地当局报告了令人不安的反常度增加，最主动的使用者们出现了严重的精神崩溃。生产力在下降，犯罪率在上升，当地民众没有表现出要戒除成瘾的迹象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:467`

### English

Use of the drug Lucid continues to rise on [Root.GetName]. Colonial authorities report a disturbing increase in deviancy, with the most active users experiencing severe psychotic breaks. Productivity is down, crime is up, and the local populace shows no signs of giving up their habit.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:539`

### English

Illegal!

## enclave.5015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

偶尔做梦

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:470`

### English

Perchance to Dream

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:543`

## enclave.5015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的科学家在研究“清醒”药物方面有了突破。这种化合物对使用者的身体系统的作用不仅是能促进清醒梦，还能使人产生化学依赖性。每服一次药，对药物的需求就更大。当给药剂量足够大时，就可以观察到大脑内部的量子波动。这些波动与我们对虚境的仅有认知恰好相符。\n\n醒来后，研究对象抱怨出现头痛和幻觉。极端情况会导致致命的大出血。随后的尸检显示，其脑部质量净增加了0.01%。梦本身似乎留下了某种痕迹。\n\n该项目负责人正在申请额外的资源，以更好地了解药物的作用。幸运的是，考虑到对这种化合物的需求，我们并不缺乏志愿者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:471`

### English

Our scientists have made a breakthrough in their study of the drug Lucid. The compound's effect on a user's system not only promotes lucid dreaming, but also precipitates a chemical dependency. The need becomes greater each time the drug is taken. When administered at high enough dosages, quantum fluctuations have been observed within the brain. These fluctuations are consistent with what little we know of the Shroud.\n\nAfter waking, subjects complained of headaches and hallucinations. Extreme cases result in fatal hemorrhaging. Subsequent autopsies reveal a net .01% increase in brain mass. The dreams themselves are apparently leaving behind some kind of residue.\n\nThe project's lead is requesting additional resources to better understand the drug's effects. Fortunately, given the demand for the compound, we have no shortage of volunteers.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:544`

## enclave.5020.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5020.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对药物“清醒”的研究得出了令人不安的结果：几个研究对象在长期使用后死亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:481`

### English

Disturbing results from our study of the drug Lucid: several research subjects have died after prolonged use.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:555`

## enclave.5020.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5020.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

足足有30%的人在脑干中长出了肿瘤。高倍成像显示这些肿瘤是某种寄生虫的幼虫阶段。\n\n对其的理论解释为，药物产生的量子涨落让这些寄生虫得以在相邻的维度平面之间“窜越”。显然，我们的神经结构非常适合它们的饮食需求。\n\n自从这些发现被公开后，安全部队报告说与“清醒”有关的逮捕减少了50%。也许我们的人民终于醒来了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:482`

### English

Fully 30% developed a tumorous growth in their brainstems. High-magnification imaging revealed these tumors to be the larval stage of some form of parasite.\n\nIt's theorized that the quantum fluctuations produced by the drug allowed these parasites to "cross over" from an adjacent dimensional plane. Apparently, our neural makeup is perfectly suited to their dietary needs.\n\nSince these findings went public, security forces report 50% fewer Lucid-related arrests. Perhaps our people are finally waking up.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:556`

### English

### English

## enclave.5025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

来自[Root.GetName]的令人不安的消息：越来越多的死亡报告从使用药物“清醒”的人群中涌现。这种化合物似乎不仅能促进欢欣性做梦，还能促成一种化学依赖。高剂量会导致内出血，最终导致死亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:485`

### English

Disturbing news from [Root.GetName]: an increasing number of fatalities have been reported among users of the drug Lucid. It seems the compound not only promotes euphoric dreaming, but also precipitates a chemical dependency. High enough doses result in internal hemorrhaging and eventually, death.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:560`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5025.a&lang=en)

### English

Infuriating.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:561`

## enclave.5030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5030.name&lang=en)

### 中文

唯梦是求

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:487`

### English

They Cry to Dream Again

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:563`

## enclave.5030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]继续广泛使用药物“清醒”。尽管罪犯会受到严厉的惩罚，但越来越多的人开始认为梦境中的欢欣状态比醒来的生活更可取。即使结果是自己的死亡，他们也不会被吓倒。[Root.GetName]的稳定度继续下降。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:488`

### English

Widespread use of the drug Lucid continues on [Root.GetName]. Despite the harsh penalties being meted out to offenders, a growing number of our people have come to view the euphoric dream state as preferable to waking life. Even if the result is their own death, they will not be deterred. Stability on [Root.GetName] continues to decline.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:564`

## enclave.5035.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5035.name&lang=en)

### 中文

死亡之眠

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:489`

### English

That Sleep of Death

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:566`

## enclave.5035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]与“清醒”相关的死亡人数继续上升。然而，随着社会条件的恶化，越来越多的人转向毒品以寻求逃避。如果事情继续这样下去，我们有失去殖民地的危险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:490`

### English

The number of Lucid-related deaths on [Root.GetName] continue to rise. However, as societal conditions worsen, more and more people are turning to the drug for escape. If things continue like this, we are in danger of losing the colony.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:567`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5035.a&lang=en)

### English

## enclave.5075.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5075.name&lang=en)

### 中文

流浪的萨满

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:502`

### English

Rogue Shaman

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:581`

## enclave.5075.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5075.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一位[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]的流亡者来到了[Root.GetName]。由于信奉异端思想，这位“极端主义者”从城邦中被流放了出来，结果没有在任何到来的船只上登记显现。它直接出现在殖民地大型城市的中央广场，就好像空气突然物质化了一样，然后公开地宣告自己的信条能让它同虚境建立个人通讯。根据这个流亡者的说法，我们的人民可以通过共享梦境实现一种赐福状态。报告指出，殖民地里有很多人都听信了这个说法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:503`

### English

An exile from the [shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName] has arrived on [Root.GetName]. Recently expelled from the Enclave for harbouring heretical views, this 'extremist' was not listed on the manifest of any incoming vessel. As if materializing out of the air, they appeared in the central square of the colony's largest city, publicly proclaiming a doctrine of personal communion with the Shroud. According to the exile, it is possible for our people to achieve a state of bliss akin to communal dreaming. Reports suggest that many in the colony are listening.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:582`

## enclave.5075.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5075.a&lang=en)

### 中文

极端主义者在这里没有一席之地。把这个流亡者弄走。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:504`

### English

Extremists have no place here. Remove the exile.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:583`

## enclave.5075.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5075.b&lang=en)

### 中文

对拜访视而不见是不明智的。好好盯着这个流亡者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:505`

### English

Ignoring a visitation is unwise. Keep an eye on this exile.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:584`

## enclave.5075.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5075.c&lang=en)

### 中文

以宾客的规格欢迎这位流亡者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:506`

### English

Welcome the exile as a guest.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:585`

## enclave.5076.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5076.name&lang=en)

### 中文

宗教狂热

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:508`

### English

Religious Fervor

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:587`

## enclave.5076.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5076.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们做了最大的努力，在[Root.GetName]上行动的那个[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]流亡分子依然在躲避着我们。或许是拥有什么先天的灵能能力，它总是可以在我们的安保部队到达之前进行预判。除此之外，流亡者的布道还在吸引大量的群众。许多殖民者都放弃工作，以响应这个所谓的萨满所拥护的洗礼和冥想实践。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:509`

### English

Despite our best efforts, the exiled member of the [shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName] operating on [Root.GetName] continues to elude us. Perhaps due to some innate psionic ability, they have consistently been able to predict the arrival of our security forces. What's more, the exile's sermons continue to draw large crowds. Many colonists have begun to abandon their work in favor of the various ablutions and meditative practices espoused by this so-called shaman.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:588`

## enclave.5076.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5076.a&lang=en)

### 中文

必须想办法阻止这个流亡者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:510`

### English

The exile must be stopped.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:589`

## enclave.5077.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5077.name&lang=en)

### 中文

殉道者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

### English

The Martyr

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:591`

## enclave.5077.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5077.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上的安保部队成功地消灭了那个流亡的萨满。这个流放者前信徒中的一名——一位没能实现应许的梦境状态而逐渐心生不满的殖民者——向我们提供了必要的情报。普罗大众似乎接受了现实，然而，一小撮顽固分子似乎仍然抱守着萨满的教义。到目前为止，对这个殖民地增添安保人员似乎是有道理的，这样可以让人们对这位“殉道者”的尊敬逐渐消散褪去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:513`

### English

Security forces on [Root.GetName] have successfully eliminated the rogue shaman. One of the exile's former adherents - a colonist who has grown increasingly disaffected by their failure to achieve the promised dream state - has provided us with the necessary intel. While the general populace appears to have accepted reality, an obstinate few remain dedicated to the shaman's teachings. For now, an increased security presence on the colony seems advisable, lest the veneration of this 'martyr' continue to fester.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:592`

## enclave.5077.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5077.a&lang=en)

### 中文

就这样吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:514`

### English

So be it.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:593`

## enclave.5080.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5080.name&lang=en)

### 中文

织梦者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:516`

### English

Dream Weaver

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:595`

## enclave.5080.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5080.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

流亡萨满的教义继续在[Root.GetName]上的人群内传播。这种激进的教条是某种能够让个人同虚境沟通的宗派，其践行者宣称能够实现一种改变意识的状态。我们收到许多报告，称极乐与康宁的感觉有所提升。其中一些人甚至宣称，通过某种共享的、梦境一般的状态，他们能够使用灵能同其他人沟通。不幸的是，这些“真信者”引发了一波创纪录的离职潮。生产力受到了很大的打击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:517`

### English

The teachings of the exiled shaman on [Root.GetName] continue to spread throughout the populace. This radical doctrine is one of personal communion with the Shroud, and its practitioners claim to have achieved an altered state of consciousness. We are receiving reports of increased feelings of euphoria and well-being. Some individuals even claim they are now able to psionically communicate with one another through a shared, dreamlike state. Unfortunately, these 'true believers' are also leaving their jobs in record numbers. Productivity has taken a hit.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:596`

## enclave.5080.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5080.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这件事到此为止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:518`

### English

Enough of this.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:597`

## enclave.5080.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5080.b&lang=en)

### 中文

幸福就是它的嘉奖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:519`

### English

Happiness is its own reward.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:598`

## enclave.5081.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5081.name&lang=en)

### 中文

突然飞升

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:521`

### English

Sudden Ascent

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:600`

## enclave.5081.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5081.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们位于[Root.GetName]的殖民地传来了惊人的新闻：那个流亡萨满宣称自己的业绩已经完成，明显已经离开了殖民地。报告很零散，且并不明确，但所有报告都同意一点，即在带领信徒进行了最后一次冥想之后，这位萨满就那样从殖民地的中心广场凭空消失了。对于那些信徒来说，这就是萨满“飞升”进入虚境的证据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:522`

### English

Startling news from our colony on [Root.GetName]: proclaiming their work done, the exiled shaman has apparently left the colony. Reports are scattered and inconclusive, but they all agree that after leading their followers in a final meditation, the shaman simply vanished from the colony's central square. To their followers, this is evidence of the shaman's 'ascension' into the Shroud.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:601`

## enclave.5082.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5082.name&lang=en)

### 中文

灵能交流

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:524`

### English

Psionic Communion

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:603`

## enclave.5082.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5082.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的事态有了奇怪的进展：在那个流亡萨满进行了所谓的“飞升”之后，我们的殖民者报告他们经历了一种意识的转换状态。这些人继续遵从萨满所推崇的仪式，现在已经能够进入一种灵能交流的状态。这种状态在殖民者间涌现出极大的欢愉和宁和，产生了深远的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:525`

### English

Strange developments on [Root.GetName]: after the so-called 'ascension' of the exiled shaman, our colonists are reporting the experience of an altered state of consciousness. Continuing to follow the practices espoused by the shaman, these individuals are able to enter a state of psionic communion. The outpouring of joy and well-being this has produced amongst the colonists is profound.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:604`

## enclave.5090.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5090.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大分裂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:533`

### English

The Great Schism

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:613`

## enclave.5090.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5090.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一艘载满了来自[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]的流亡侍僧的运输船同我们进行了联络。他们称自己为“变革派”，要求在我们的某颗星球上进行难民庇护。\n\n这一事件意味着虚境行者内部发生了前所未有的大分裂。看来，太空城邦也没有我们想象中的那么团结。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

We have been contacted by a transport ship carrying a number of exiled acolytes from the [shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]. Calling themselves 'Reformers', they are requesting refuge on one of our worlds.\n\nThis represents an unprecedented schism within the Shroudwalkers. It would appear the Enclave is not as united as we were led to believe.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:614`

## enclave.5090.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5090.a&lang=en)

欢迎他们来这。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

They are welcome here.

异种在我国境内没有容身之地。

## enclave.5095.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5095.name&lang=en)

### 中文

城邦发言

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:539`

### English

The Enclave Speaks

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:619`

## enclave.5095.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5095.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“虚境惊诧。我们收到消息，听说我们的一些前侍僧已经作为难民在你的星球安顿下来。这一作为让我们极其不悦，但就这样吧。鲁莽的行动一般都会导致绝望的后果。但是要记住这点：这些改革派不过是一群坑蒙拐骗之徒，一群无知的蠢蛋，以为自己掌握着破解虚境秘密的钥匙。庇护这群异端会让你冒风险。那些居住在虚境里的存在肯定会注意到这件事。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:540`

### English

"The Shroud trembles. Word has reached us that some our former acolytes have taken refuge on one of your worlds. This has displeased us greatly, but so be it. Reckless action often leads to desperate results. Know this: these Reformers are nothing more than charlatans, vain fools who believe they hold the key to unlocking the secrets of the Shroud. Shelter such heretics at your peril. Those that dwell within the Shroud have surely taken notice."

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:620`

## enclave.5100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

门与钥匙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:541`

### English

The Door and the Key

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:621`

## enclave.5100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“我们的恩主！在其他那么多世界置我们生死于不顾的时候，是你为我们提供了庇护……虚境一定会保佑你的，不论是此世还是彼世。我们的前同胞，[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]被传统遮蔽了双眼。虚境并非是不可渗透的。它存在一扇门，而我们发现了它的钥匙。\n\n为了报答你的慷慨，我们愿意同你分享这个秘密。我们唯一要求的就是开工建设所需要的物资。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:542`

### English

"Our benefactors! That you have sheltered us, when so many others would have turned their backs... Surely the Shroud will bless you, both in this life and in that which lies Beyond. Our former brethren, the [shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName], are blinded by tradition. The Shroud is not impermeable. There is a Door, and we have discovered its Key.\n\nIn reward for your generosity, we would share these secrets with you. All we require are the resources with which to begin construction."

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:622`

## enclave.5100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会给你们的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

You shall have them.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:623`

## enclave.5100.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5100.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这价格太高了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:544`

### English

The price is too steep.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:624`

## enclave.5105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

钥匙完工

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:545`

### English

The Key is Fashioned

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:625`

## enclave.5105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“虚境惊诧！有了贵方的帮助，我们最终完成了大钥匙的建设。现在，我们最有天赋的同胞聚集在一起准备将其打开。随着他们的意识同钥匙连接在一起，他们的灵能能力得到了强化。此刻，这些勇敢的朝圣者进入了一个共享类梦境状态。当他们苏醒的时候，一扇前往虚境的大门就会打开。\n\n愿那些居住的虚境中的存在为他们的努力赐福。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:546`

### English

"The Shroud shivers! With your help, we have at last completed construction of the Great Key. Even now, our most gifted brethren have gathered to begin the Opening. With their minds tethered to the Key, their psionic abilities have been amplified. Now, these brave pilgrims enter a shared dreamlike state. At their awakening, a Door to the Shroud will open.\n\nMay those that dwell within the Shroud bless their efforts."

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:626`

## enclave.5106.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5106.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境弥漫的星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:547`

### English

Shroud-Scented World

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:627`

## enclave.5106.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5106.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

令人担忧的消息自我们位于[Root.GetName]的殖民地传来：尽管缺乏资源，某些狂热的改革分子依然试图自己锻造了钥匙。他们使用临时替代装置增强自己的灵能能力，进入了共享类梦境状态。这一举动造成了殖民地中心城市的上方出现了一个时空裂口。有毒的、诱发焦虑的烟气瞬间灌了出来，看上去是来自虚境本身。许多人将这一现象称之为“虚境恶臭”，并且我们被告知这样的体验并不愉悦。[Root.GetName]的民心普遍的降低了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:548`

### English

Disturbing news from our colony on [Root.GetName]: despite the lack of resources, some of the more zealous Reformers attempted to fashion a Key themselves. By amplifying their psionic abilities with the makeshift device, they entered into a shared dreamlike state. This precipitated a breach in the fabric of spacetime above the colony's central city. Noxious, anxiety-inducing fumes are issuing forth, presumably from the Shroud itself. Many are calling this phenomenon 'Shroud Stench', and we are told the experience is far from pleasant. Morale on [Root.GetName] is at an all-time low.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:628`

## great_schism_03

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agreat_schism_03&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境恶臭

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:549`

### English

Shroud Stench

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:629`

## enclave.5110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大门洞开

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:550`

### English

The Door Opens

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:630`

## enclave.5110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们位于[Root.GetName]的殖民地传来了令人难以置信的好消息：改革派们从长达数月的出神状态中苏醒了过来，现在已经成功地打开了一扇通往虚境的大门。一道靓丽的时空裂缝出现在了殖民地的中心城市上方。现在，某种类似强风的存在涌了出来，奶白色的亮光将天空渲染得五颜六色。刮过殖民地的灵能共振透过了改革派和殖民者的心以及意识。许多人相信这是[Root.GetName]“黄金时代”的黎明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:551`

### English

Incredible news from our colony on [Root.GetName]: awakening from their months-long trance, the Reformers have succeeded in opening a doorway to the Shroud. A brilliant rift in spacetime has appeared above the colony's central city. Now, something like a great wind is issuing forth, iridescent with milky light. The psionic resonance that has blown through the colony has permeated the hearts and minds of Reformers and colonists alike. Many believe that this is the dawn a new 'golden age' on [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:631`

## great_schism_01

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agreat_schism_01&lang=en)

### 中文

黄金时代

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:552`

### English

A Golden Age

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:632`

## enclave.5115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们位于[Root.GetName]的殖民地传来了灾难般的坏消息：改革派们从长达数月的出神状态中苏醒了过来，现在已经成功地打开了一扇通往虚境的大门。一道破败的时空裂缝出现在了殖民地的中心城市上方。现在，某种类似纱雾的存在涌了出来，邪恶的亮光将天空渲染得十分瘆人。刮过殖民地的灵能瘴气扭曲了改革派和殖民者的心以及意识。许多人相信这是[Root.GetName]“黑暗时代”的开端。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:553`

### English

Disastrous news from our colony on [Root.GetName]: awakening from their months-long trance, the Reformers have succeeded in opening a doorway to the Shroud. A ragged rift in spacetime has appeared above the colony's central city. Now, something like a sickly fog is issuing forth, lurid with vile light. The psionic miasma that has permeated the colony has twisted the hearts and minds of Reformers and colonists alike. Many view this as the beginning of a new 'dark age' on [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:633`

## great_schism_02

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agreat_schism_02&lang=en)

### 中文

黑暗时代

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:554`

### English

An Age of Darkness

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:634`

## enclave.5120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“虚境惊诧。我们已经收到消息，你拒绝了我们的前侍僧在你的国境内寻求庇护的请求。你做的很明智。这些‘改革派’不过是一群坑蒙拐骗之徒；一群无知的蠢蛋，以为自己掌握着破解虚境秘密的钥匙。任何决定庇护这群异端的团体都会冒风险。就让他们在太空中飘荡吧，无家可归，无心无肺，永远被此世和彼世遗忘。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:556`

### English

"The Shroud shivers. Word has reached us that you have denied our former acolytes refuge within your borders. You are wise. These 'Reformers' are nothing more than charlatans; vain fools who believe they hold the key to unlocking the mysteries of the Shroud. Any who choose to shelter such heretics do so at their peril. Let them wander the void, homeless, hearthless, forever forsaken in this world and the next."

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:636`

## enclave.5130.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

漂浮的船壳

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:558`

### English

Floating Shell

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:639`

## enclave.5130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

长程传感器检测到一个大体积的无法鉴别身份的中空残骸突然出现的[floating_shell_system.GetName]星系。尽管我们对其设计很陌生，这个物体的组成成分中含有稀有的矿物，显示其建造过程中使用了先进的科技。物体的内侧表面包含了几百个接口和附着点，显然是为缺失的部分预留的。\n\n就如同这个物体是随机地出现在[floating_shell_system.GetName]星系一样，它的内部部分就好像单纯地消失了一样。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:559`

### English

Long-range sensors have detected the sudden appearance of an unidentified chunk of hollow debris in the [floating_shell_system.GetName] system. While alien in design, the object's rare-mineral composition suggests the use of advanced technology in its construction. The inner surface contains hundreds of ports and appendages that appear intended for lacking parts.\n\nJust as the object appeared in the [floating_shell_system.GetName] system at random, it is as if the missing interior simply vanished.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:640`

## FLOATING_PARTS_PROJECT_00

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFLOATING_PARTS_PROJECT_00&lang=en)

### 中文

研究船壳

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:560`

### English

Study Shell

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:642`

## FLOATING_PARTS_PROJECT_00_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFLOATING_PARTS_PROJECT_00_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这个看上去像是异星人舰船的外壳被发现漂浮在[floating_shell_system.GetName]星系。它是怎么到达那里的，这艘舰船的其他部分在哪里？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:561`

### English

The shell of what looks like an alien vessel was found floating in the [floating_shell_system.GetName] system. How did it get there, and where is the rest of the ship?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:643`

## enclave.5135.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5135.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被放弃的船壳

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:562`

### English

Abandoned Hull

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:645`

## enclave.5135.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5135.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对在[floating_shell_system.GetName]被我们发现的船壳进行了进一步的分析，确认了它是外星人舰船设计的一个样本。尽管没有动力，这个船壳本身有一些独特的特征，最有可能是一种先进形式的装甲覆盖。\n\n然而，我们依然对于这个船壳来源于哪里、如何以及为什么出现在我们的宇域、它的内部（如果有的话）发生了什么毫无头绪。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:563`

### English

Further analysis of the shell discovered in the [floating_shell_system.GetName] system has confirmed it as an example of alien ship design. While lacking power, the hull itself possesses several unique characteristics, most notably an advanced form of armored cladding.\n\nHowever, we are no closer to understanding where the hull originated from, how or why it appeared in our space, and what (if anything) happened to its interior.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:646`

## enclave.5136.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5136.name&lang=en)

### 中文

漂浮的碎片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:564`

### English

Floating Fragment

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:648`

## enclave.5136.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5136.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

传感器检测到了又一个大体积的外星残骸，这一次是出现在[floating_engine_system.GetName]星系。这个物体在设计上同先前发现的外星人船壳很相似，其配置和错综复杂的结构表明它是某种引擎核心。安置在内部的是一个独特的装置，我们将其暂时称为一个“灵能调制器”。这个古怪的装置能让这艘舰船同虚境的灵能能量进行互动。尽管同我们的科技无法兼容，我们对其开展进一步的研究或许可以得到喜出望外的结果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:565`

### English

Sensors have detected the appearance of another chunk of alien debris, this time in the [floating_engine_system.GetName] system. Similar in design to the recently discovered alien hull, the configuration and intricacy of the object suggests some type of engine core. Nestled inside was a unique apparatus that we have dubbed a 'psionic modulator.' This strange device would have allowed the ship to interact with psionic energy of the Shroud. While incompatible with our technology, further study may yield fascinating results.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:649`

## enclave.5156.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5156.a&lang=en)

### 中文

解开谜题的拼图又多了一片。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:566`

### English

Another piece of the puzzle.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:650`

## enclave.5140.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5140.name&lang=en)

### 中文

漂浮的核心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:567`

### English

Floating Core

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:652`

## enclave.5140.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5140.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

另一个在我们的宇域内随机出现的外星舰船部件被我们发现了，这一次是在[floating_core_system.GetName]星系。其中包括有条纹的甲板、没有墙的船员舱室以及一个先进电脑核心，毫无疑问这肯定是舰船船壳上面消失的内部部分。一旦全部三个部件都被集中到了一起，我们的工程师应该可以将这艘舰船组装成完整的状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:568`

### English

Another component from the alien vessel that has been appearing at random throughout our space has been detected in the [floating_core_system.GetName] system. Consisting of striated decks, unwalled crew-quarters, and an advanced computer core, there is no doubt that this must have comprised the bulk of the vessel's missing interior. Once all three components are brought together, our engineers should be able to assemble the ship in its entirety.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:653`

The puzzle takes shape.
