## FLOATING_PARTS_PROJECT_01_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFLOATING_PARTS_PROJECT_01_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

外星舰船的最后一部分出现在了[floating_core_system.GetName]星系。我们的工程师需要时间重新组装这艘舰船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:571`

### English

The final piece of the alien ship has appeared in the [floating_core_system.GetName]. Our engineers need time to reassemble the vessel.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:657`

## enclave.5145.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5145.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境碰触的舰船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:572`

### English

Shroud-Touched Vessel

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:659`

## enclave.5145.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5145.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经成功地重新组装了这艘外星舰船。随着计算机核心上线，我们有可能下载和翻译舰船的日志。看上去这艘舰船还属于实验型号，设计目的是通过虚境的手段实现超光速旅行。尽管最后的船员日志和外部遥感勘测数据依然被扭曲，但是有一点很确定，那就是这艘舰船在处女航时出了什么大的差错。这艘舰船并不是因为爆炸解体，而是其部件被随机地扔回了正常空间。船员的命运尚且未知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:573`

### English

We have succeeded in reassembling the alien vessel. With the computer core online, it was possible to download and translate the ship's logs. It seems the vessel was an experimental prototype, designed to achieve faster-than-light travel by means of the Shroud. Although the final crew logs and external telemetry remain garbled, there is no doubt that something went badly wrong on the vessel's maiden voyage. The ship was not so much blown apart as disassembled, its component pieces spat out at random into normal space. The fate of the crew remains unknown.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:660`

## enclave.5145.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5145.a&lang=en)

### 中文

将这艘舰船编入我们的舰队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:574`

### English

Add the vessel to our fleet.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:661`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5145.b&lang=en)

把它拆成零件。

### English

## enclave.5150.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5150.name&lang=en)

### 中文

灵能异常现象

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:577`

### English

Psionic Anomaly

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:665`

## enclave.5150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

传感器发布了紧急预报，探测到了一个先前未曾标记的异常现象。考虑到这个事件中刚刚记录到的前所未有的灵能能量峰值，我们毫不怀疑这是一次和虚境的连结。这就是[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]预知的改变吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:578`

### English

Sensors have detected the emergence of a previously uncharted anomaly. Given the incredible spike in psionic energy registered just before the event, there is almost certainly a connection to the Shroud. Could this be the change foretold by the [shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:666`

## enclave.5195.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5195.name&lang=en)

### 中文

英灵再现

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:580`

### English

Hero Redivivus

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:670`

## enclave.5195.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5195.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]传来了难以置信的新闻：今天早晨，某个宣称[hero_redivivus.GetHimselfHerself]是传奇英雄§Y[hero_redivivus.GetName]§!的个体来到了首都的大广场。目击者们说感到了一股出于敬畏的沉默，强大的冷静和宁和之感席卷了聚集起来的民众。尽管[hero_redivivus.GetName]在历史上人尽皆知，是一名伟大的雄辩家和充满激情的领袖，这名可疑的个体用单调的、几乎没有感情的腔调，诉说着自己在“死后世界”遇到的经历。此人宣称自己的返回并不是为了领导，而是为了抹除我们对死亡的恐惧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:581`

### English

Incredible news from [Root.GetName]: early this morning, an individual proclaiming [hero_redivivus.GetHimselfHerself] to be the legendary hero §Y[hero_redivivus.GetName]§! entered the great square of the capital. Witnesses describe scenes of awed silence, as powerful feelings of calm and wellbeing radiated throughout the gathering crowd. While [hero_redivivus.GetName] was known to history as a great orator and impassioned leader, the individual in question spoke of their supposed experience 'in the afterworld' in a simple, almost lighthearted tone. They claim to have returned not to lead, but to erase our fear of death.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:671`

### 中文

这会不会是个骗局？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:582`

### English

Could it be a hoax?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:672`

Hero or Hoax?

## REDIVIVUS_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AREDIVIVUS_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

某个宣称自己是一名传奇英雄的个体来到了我们的母星。这样的暴论有可能是真的吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:584`

### English

An individual claiming to be a legendary hero has appeared on our homeworld. Could their wild assertions possibly be true?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:674`

## enclave.5200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境碰触的英雄

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:585`

### English

Shroud-Touched Hero

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:676`

## enclave.5200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对那名宣称自己是§Y[hero_redivivus.GetName]§!的个体进行的调查有了结论。一开始我们的研究者们保持了可以理解的怀疑态度，然而这名个体不仅展现出了对伟大英雄所在时代和当地文化的极其详尽的知识，DNA分析也确认此人和伟大英雄的数个已知后代存在基因上的联系。\n\n不仅如此，对[hero_redivivus.GetName]脑电波图样的更深层研究表明，其大脑存在独特的灵能残留。看上去，与其说是“从死亡重返人间”，[hero_redivivus.GetName]更像是以某种方式被传送到了一个类似于虚境的位面。[hero_redivivus.GetHisHerCap]对自己的“来世”经历显然很满意，[hero_redivivus.GetHisHer]现在安顿在首都郊区一处很安静的地方，并向愿意听从此人的群众布道，传播和平与无惧的信条。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:586`

### English

The investigation into the individual claiming to be §Y[hero_redivivus.GetName]§! has concluded. At first our researchers were understandably skeptical, but not only did the subject possess incredibly detailed knowledge of the great hero's time period and culture, DNA analysis confirms a genetic link to several known descendants.\n\nMoreover, closer study of [hero_redivivus.GetName]'s' brainwave pattern revealed a distinct psionic residue. It seems likely that far from being 'returned from death', [hero_redivivus.GetName] was in some way transported to a realm akin the Shroud. [hero_redivivus.GetHisHerCap] experience of this 'afterlife' was apparently a contented one, and [hero_redivivus.GetHisHer] has now settled into a quiet existence on the outskirts of the capital, preaching a doctrine of fearlessness and peace to any who will listen.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:677`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5200.a&lang=en)

奇怪的命运。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:587`

### English

A strange fate.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:678`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahero_redivivus&lang=en)

安享余年的英灵

### English

Hero at Rest

## enclave.5500.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5500.name&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道上的断裂处

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:590`

### English

A Rupture in Orbit

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:683`

## enclave.5500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

许多我们安置在[shattered_frontier_planet.GetName]轨道上的人造卫星最近被大幅度地调整了。依据他们的传感器日志，一个§Y稳定而定语的重力井§!在我们行星轨道之上形成了。\n\n地表的观测数据证实这个异常现象是突然出现的，而我们对其成因一无所知。重力井暂时对我们的殖民地似乎没有威胁，但卫星用来维持轨道所需要消耗的燃料过多，很快将难以为继。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:591`

### English

Many of the satellites we placed in orbit of [shattered_frontier_planet.GetName] have had to make significant adjustments in recent days. According to their sensor logs, a §Ystable and localized gravity well§! has formed in orbit above the planet.\n\nData from the surface confirms that the anomaly appeared suddenly, though we do not know how or why. The gravity well doesn't seem to pose any inherent threat to our colony for now, but satellites are having to expend unsustainable amounts of fuel in order to adjust their orbits around it.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:684`

## enclave.5500.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5500.a&lang=en)

### 中文

发起一次调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:592`

### English

Launch an investigation.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:685`

## SHATTERED_FRONTIER_ANOMALY

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHATTERED_FRONTIER_ANOMALY&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道断裂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:593`

### English

Orbital Rupture

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:686`

## SHATTERED_FRONTIER_ANOMALY_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHATTERED_FRONTIER_ANOMALY_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

一处怪异的引力井突然出现在[shattered_frontier_planet.GetName]的上空，扰乱了许多我们的人造卫星轨道。殖民地本身尚未受到威胁，但这一现象出现在我们的一处殖民点附近，还是造成了不便。\n\n此外，如果我们不加干预，许多在[shattered_frontier_planet.GetName]上空运转的卫星将面临着提前坠落的风险。一旦发生，显然这将会实实在在地妨碍我们研究新生成的重力井的能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:594`

### English

A strange gravity well suddenly appeared above [shattered_frontier_planet.GetName], disrupting many of our satellite orbits. The colony itself doesn't seem to be under threat yet, but this phenomenon is situated uncomfortably close to one of our colonies.\n\nFurthermore, many of the satellites we placed above [shattered_frontier_planet.GetName] face an early de-orbiting if we do not intervene soon. Should that happen, it would significantly hamper our ability to study the new gravity well in detail.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:687`

## enclave.5501.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5501.name&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道断层坍塌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:595`

### English

Orbital Rupture Collapses

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:689`

## enclave.5501.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5501.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetName]上的研究人员告知，重力井异常已经坍塌了，坍塌产生的电力瘫痪引起了附近许多的诊断设施和附近的卫星的故障。这一事件似乎就在我们停止观测的一刻发生，许多人认为这是一个令人不安的巧合。\n\n[root.GetName]上的殖民者也汇报了大规模的硬件故障，尽管只有少数和轨道上的冲击波直接相关，而殖民地的其他人则不得不与异常疯狂的暴徒打交道，这些暴徒自发地地破坏了关键设施。当地官员对这些暴乱的自发性质感到困惑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:596`

### English

Researchers on [root.GetName] inform us that the gravity well anomaly has collapsed, causing electrical failures in many of our diagnostic devices and nearby satellites. This event seems to have occurred the moment we ceased our observations; something many regard as an unnerving coincidence.\n\nThe colonists on [root.GetName] also report widespread hardware failures, though only some of this can be linked to the orbital blast. Instead, the rest of our colony is having to deal with unusually chaotic mobs who have taken it upon themselves to vandalize key facilities. Local officials are baffled by the spontaneous nature of these riots.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:690`

## enclave.5501.gestalt.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5501.gestalt.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetName]上空的重力井异常发生了猛烈的坍塌，造成地面上和附近人造卫星的电力瘫痪。这一事件似乎就在我们停止观测的一刻发生，并最终引起了一场在轨全频段无线电爆。\n\n虽然对我们硬件设施的影响还在可控范围之内，但这异常的无线电爆对我们的一些子个体产生了影响。我们……我们频繁地与它们失去联系。仿佛[root.GetName]沉浸在了某种致密的蒸汽当中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:597`

### English

The gravity well anomaly over [root.GetName] has violently collapsed, causing electrical failures on the ground and amongst nearby satellites. This event seems to have occurred the moment we ceased our observations, culminating in an orbital shockwave across multiple radiation bands.\n\nWhile disruptions to our hardware have so far proven quite manageable, this unusual radiation appears to cause some interference amongst our drones. We cannot... sense them quite so fluently. It is as though [root.GetName] has been immersed in some form of dense vapor.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:691`

## shattered_frontier_looting

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashattered_frontier_looting&lang=en)

### 中文

趁火打劫

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:598`

### English

Post-Anomaly Looting

异常反常

### English

Post-Anomaly Deviancy

## enclave.5502.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5502.name&lang=en)

### 中文

裂缝显现

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:600`

### English

Cracks Appear

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:695`

## enclave.5502.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5502.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[root.GetName]的殖民地已经陷入了更进一步的混乱中，另有一百多名殖民者突然失踪。“零元购”和动乱仍在继续当中，但范围有所缩小。我们推测只是对更多的失踪的恐惧让他们的势头有所收敛。到目前为止，每个消失的人都在返回各自住所的路上。\n\n偏执狂、阴谋论甚至幻觉的报道都在增加——但现实可能更令人不安。[divination_situation.GetName]确实灵验了，但我们是否让[root.GetName]引起了一些无形且险恶的§H灵能实体§!的注意呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:601`

### English

Our colony on [root.GetName] has fallen further into disarray, with the sudden disappearance of over a hundred colonists. Gratuitous looting and riots continue, though no longer in such a widespread manner. We suspect their zeal is being tempered only through fear of further disappearances. In every case so far, those who vanished had been on their way back to their respective dwellings.\n\nParanoia, conspiracy theories and even reports of hallucinations are on the rise - but the reality may be more unsettling. The [divination_situation.GetName] is clearly in effect, but have we brought [root.GetName] to the attention of some intangible and sinister §Hpsionic entity§!?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:696`

## enclave.5502.gestalt.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5502.gestalt.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[root.GetName]上的殖民地正在遭受攻击！我们已经永久失去了一部分子个体的联系。它们被从我们当中抹除了。事情发生得突然又彻底，没有任何暴力发生的迹象……打击来得如此迅速我们甚至来不及看到到底是如何发生的。\n\n殖民地的许多核心功能都受到了影响。更多的子个体正争先恐后地填补空缺，但我们显然已经受到了反作用。恢复[root.GetName]上的平衡还需要一些时间。\n\n[divination_situation.GetName]显然灵验了，但它现在是否将我们置于某种无形且险恶的§H灵能实体§!的监视之下了呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:602`

### English

Our colony on [root.GetName] is under attack! We can no longer perceive many of our own drones. They have been... removed from us. It happened so quickly and yet so neatly, with no violence... We have been struck a blow so swift, we couldn't even see it happening.\n\nMany core functions of the colony have been interrupted. Additional drones are scrambling to take up the slack, but we have recoiled. It will take some time to regain our balance on [root.GetName]\n\nThe [divination_situation.GetName] is clearly in effect, but has it now placed us under the scrutiny of some intangible and sinister §Hpsionic entity§!?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:697`

### 中文

晴朗时分

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:619`

### English

Moment of Clarity

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:715`

## enclave.5503.looting.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5503.looting.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

负责监视我们位于[root.GetName]的受损殖民地的官员惊愕地汇报道——尽管我们没有采取任何救灾措施——当地已经恢复了正常秩序。他们形容道，当地的局势就好像，[root.GetName]的一切都在一场漫长的围攻中获得了喘息。尽管没有人知道为什么，但是大家都冷静了下来。\n\n$enclave.5503.regular.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:620`

### English

Officials overseeing our stricken colony on [root.GetName] are astonished to report that - despite our taking no remedial action - normality has been restored. They describe the situation as though all of [root.GetName] has been granted respite from a prolonged siege. A calm has descended, though no-one knows why.\n\n$enclave.5503.regular.end$

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:716`

## enclave.5503.regular.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5503.regular.end&lang=en)

### 中文

许多殖民者不约而同地聚集在一起，构成了新的、更加亲密的社群。随着他们开始相互治愈伤口，人们也开始相互分享自己奇怪的幻境和挥之不去的梦魇。这些轶事在聚居者的心中助长了一种全新的使命感，现在他们看待自己的家园多了一份心生畏惧的尊敬。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:621`

### English

Many of the colonists have gathered themselves into new, closer communities. As they begin the work of mutual healing, they share their stories of strange visions and lingering nightmares. These tales have instilled a renewed sense of purpose in the settlers, who now look upon their home with fearful reverence.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:717`

## enclave.5503.benefits.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5503.benefits.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

负责监视我们位于[root.GetName]的受损殖民地的官员很乐观地报告道，当地已经恢复了正常秩序。他们形容道，当地的局势就好像，[root.GetName]的一切都在一场漫长的围攻中获得了短暂的喘息。尽管没什么人认为这一切和我们采取的措施有什么关系，但是大家都冷静了下来。\n\n$enclave.5503.regular.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:622`

### English

Officials overseeing our stricken colony on [root.GetName] are pleased to report that normality has been restored. They describe the situation as though all of [root.GetName] has been granted respite from a prolonged siege. A calm has descended, though few believe our actions actually had much to do with it.\n\n$enclave.5503.regular.end$

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:718`

## enclave.5503.lockdown.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5503.lockdown.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

负责管理[root.GetName]的封锁工作的官员如释重负地报告道，当地已经恢复了正常秩序。他们形容道，当地的局势就好像，[root.GetName]在一场漫长的围攻中获得了短暂的喘息——无论是封锁区内还是封锁区外的殖民者。不管是这些外来侵略者自己筋疲力尽了，还是说他们对此失去了兴趣，没什么人认为这一切和我们采取的措施有什么关系。\n\n许多殖民者不约而同地聚集在一起，构成了平和的、更加亲密的社群。随着他们开始相互治愈伤口，人们也开始相互分享自己奇怪的幻境和挥之不去的梦魇。许多人对于我们处理局势的手段心生怨恨，但是这些轶事在聚居者的心中助长了一种全新的使命感，现在他们看待自己的家园多了一份肃然起敬的畏惧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:623`

### English

Officials administering the lockdown on [root.GetName] are relieved to report that normality has been restored. They describe the situation as though all of [root.GetName] has been granted respite from a prolonged siege - one which the colonists have felt from within and without. Whether those outside aggressors exhausted themselves or lost interest, few believe our actions had much to do with it.\n\nMany of the colonists have gathered themselves up into docile, closer communities. As they begin the work of healing, they share their stories of strange visions and lingering nightmares. Many resent our handling of the situation, but the tales these settlers tell have instilled a renewed sense of purpose in them. They now look upon their home with fearful awe.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:719`

## enclave.5503.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5503.d&lang=en)

### 中文

终于平息了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:624`

### English

Relief at last.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:720`

## enclave.5503.gestalt.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5503.gestalt.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们位于[root.GetName]上的受损殖民地恢复了正常。然而局势仍然让人困惑：看样子是一股有腐蚀性的迷雾一直在攻击殖民地，而我们最终对这股迷雾产生了免疫效应。秩序感已经恢复，尽管我们尚未完全理解个中缘由；我们的行动和导致的结果并不一致。\n\n我们的无人机现在已经回到了日常的任务和程序，显然没有受到灵能扰动的影响。这一切的目的是什么？是那个异常现象——虚境——试图在引导我们领悟某些秘法的功课吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:625`

### English

Normality has been restored to our stricken colony on [root.GetName]. The situation is perplexing: it is as though a corrosive fog had been attacking the colony, and we have at last become immune to its effects. A sense of orderliness has returned, though we do not fully comprehend why; our actions do not fully align with this outcome.\n\nOur drones have now resumed their usual tasks and routines, unstressed by apparently psionic disturbances. What was the purpose of these? Was the anomaly - the Shroud - trying to guide us through some arcane lesson?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:721`

轨道残影

### English

## enclave.5504.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5504.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在对[root.GetName]周边的重力井进行了连续几周毫无成果的调查之后，一股不同寻常的恒星风造成的密度波动提供给了我们的科学家一个令人担忧的突破。\n\n随着[root.GetStarName]的辐射风被冲刷到异常现象地点，它们投映了一个微弱、但是毫无错误的殖民船轮廓，它的着陆器的状态杂乱无章。接着，未经证实的谣言讲道，在阴影处的废墟区有人看到了许多尸体。\n\n许多殖民者的心——包括研究小组的一部分成员——这下沉到了底。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:628`

### English

After weeks of fruitless investigation into the gravity well orbiting [root.GetName], an unusually dense wave of stellar wind has provided our scientists with a disturbing breakthrough.\n\nAs the irradiated winds from [root.GetStarName] washed over the anomaly, they cast the faint but unmistakable outline of a colony ship, its landing craft in a clear state of disarray. Further, unconfirmed rumors suggest that bodies may have been spotted amongst the shadowy debris.\n\nThe mood amongst many colonists - and some of the research team - has taken a dark turn.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:725`

## enclave.5504.gestalt.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5504.gestalt.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在对[root.GetName]周边的重力井进行了连续几周毫无成果的调查之后，一股不同寻常的恒星风造成的密度波动提供给了我们的科学家一个令人担忧的突破。\n\n随着[root.GetStarName]的辐射风被冲刷到异常现象地点，它们投映了一个微弱、但是毫无错误的殖民船轮廓，它的着陆器的状态杂乱无章。一些复杂生命体的遗骸在阴影处的废墟区四处散落着。它们的形状和我们自己十分相似。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:629`

### English

After weeks of fruitless investigation into the gravity well orbiting [root.GetName], a dense wave of stellar wind has provided us an unusual breakthrough.\n\nAs the irradiated winds from [root.GetStarName] washed over the anomaly, they cast the faint outline of a colony ship, its landing craft in a clear state of disarray. The remains of some complex lifeforms appeared strewn about the shadowy debris. Their shape seemed remarkably similar to our own.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:726`

## enclave.5505.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5505.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

汹涌的风暴在[root.GetName]引力口袋的附近爆发，这让[root.solar_system.GetName]星系陷入彻底的惊慌。所有非必要的交通都停止了，殖民地宣布进入紧急状态——所幸，到目前为止还没有遇难者的报告。\n\n我们的初步扫描全是一堆相互冲突的数据，然而当地官员却注意到，报告中提及的情感波动和“超常”活动频率在最近有了稳定的上升。这股风暴的本质有很大的可能是§H灵能§!波动。\n\n研究员们注意到殖民船的蜃影——现在位于动荡的混沌风暴中心——开始呈现出物理的轮廓。他们请求分配更多的时间来进行调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:634`

### English

A violent storm has erupted around the [root.GetName] gravity pocket, plunging the [root.solar_system.GetName] system into full-scale panic. All non-essential traffic is being diverted, and the colony has declared a state of emergency - however, no casualties have been reported so far.\n\nOur initial scans make for a mess of conflicting data, however local officials have noted a recent and steady rise in reports of emotional distress and 'paranormal' activity. It's therefore highly probable this storm is §Hpsionic§! in nature.\n\nResearchers note that the silhouetted colony ship - which lies at the centre of what is now a roiling maelstrom - has begun exhibiting physical mass. They plead for more time to investigate.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:732`

## enclave.5505.gestalt.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5505.gestalt.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

汹涌的风暴在[root.GetName]引力口袋的附近爆发，让整个殖民地陷入轻微的无序状态。我们目前没有受到伤害，然而事实证明……很难同我们在当地的无人机进行流畅的联系。\n\n我们的初步扫描全是一堆相互冲突的数据，但这股风暴的本质有很大的可能是§H灵能§!波动。我们注意到，比如说，殖民船的蜃影——现在位于动荡的混沌风暴中心——开始呈现出物理的轮廓。看来那里还有更多需要调查的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:635`

### English

A violent storm has erupted around the [root.GetName] gravity pocket, casting the colony into mild disarray. We have not yet been harmed, however it is proving... difficult to maintain truly fluent contact with our drones there.\n\nOur initial scans make for a mess of conflicting data, but it's highly probable this storm is §Hpsionic§! in nature. We note, for example, that the silhouetted colony ship - which lies at the centre of what is now a roiling maelstrom - has begun exhibiting physical mass. It seems there is more yet to investigate here.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:733`

## enclave.5505.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5505.a&lang=en)

去找你的答案——而且动作要快！

### English

Get your answers - and be quick about it!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:734`

Storm in Orbit

## shattered_frontier_storm_mod_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashattered_frontier_storm_mod_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个殖民地正在承受一个庞大风暴的冲击，风暴的本质看上去是灵能波动。当局建议一切非必要的飞行器同其保持相当的距离。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:641`

### English

This colony is bearing the brunt of a vast storm, which seems to be psionic in nature. Non-essential craft are being advised to keep well distant.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:739`

## shattered_frontier_storm_gestalt_mod

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashattered_frontier_storm_gestalt_mod&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:642`

### English

$shattered_frontier_storm_mod$

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:740`

## shattered_frontier_storm_gestalt_mod_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashattered_frontier_storm_gestalt_mod_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的殖民地正在承受一个庞大风暴的冲击，风暴的本质看上去是灵能波动。当地环境证明会对我们附近的无人机设施产生害处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:643`

### English

Our colony is being subjected to a vast storm, which seems to be psionic in nature. Conditions are proving hazardous to our nearby drone facilities.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:741`

## enclave.5506.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5506.name&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴苏醒之地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:644`

### English

In the Storm's Wake

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:743`

## enclave.5506.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5506.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不久之前，[root.GetName]遭到了一股电磁波的震荡，收到了剧烈的伤害。这股自轨道激发的灵能风暴现在已经退却了。冲击波对一大批殖民地的基础设施造成了伤害，但是维修组向我们保证此等规模的扰乱可以很快恢复正常。\n\n[root.GetName]的殖民者显然深深地被这个严酷的考验震撼，这不仅仅是因为有如此多地问题没有得到回答。我们的扫描仪没有再在[root.solar_system.GetName]星系检测到重力异常或者“幽灵船”……然而行星本身却处于不稳定的状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:645`

### English

Moments ago, [root.GetName] was rocked by the violent crack of an electromagnetic shockwave. The psionic storm which had been raging in its orbit has now retreated. A great deal of the colony's infrastructure has been damaged by this pulse, but repair crews assure us the disruption will be relatively brief.\n\nThe [root.GetName] colonists appear deeply shaken by the ordeal, not least because so many questions now go unanswered. Our scanners no longer detect gravity anomalies or 'ghost ships' in the [root.solar_system.GetName] system... However the planet itself now feels unsettled.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:744`

## enclave.5506.gestalt.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5506.gestalt.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetName]遭到了一股电磁波的震荡，受到了剧烈的伤害。这股狂暴肆虐的灵能风暴现在已经退却了。风暴对一大批殖民地的基础设施造成了伤害，但是目前为止维修工作没有遇到复杂的难题。\n\n我们的子个体行动依然缓慢，但是除此之外没有受到伤害。我们的扫描仪没有再在[root.solar_system.GetName]星系检测到重力异常或者“幽灵船”……然而，就好像依然有一股迷雾萦绕在[root.GetName]的子个体上，我们现在被巨量的无法回答的问题压得喘不过气来，

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:646`

### English

[root.GetName] has been rocked by the violent crack of an electromagnetic shockwave as the raging psionic storm has now retreated. Much of the colony's infrastructure has been damaged by this storm, but repairs have so far proven to be uncomplicated.\n\nOur drones remain sluggish but otherwise unharmed, and scanners no longer detect gravity anomalies or 'ghost ships' in the [root.solar_system.GetName] system... However, just as a fog remains over our drones on [root.GetName], so too are we now burdened by the weight of unanswered questions.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:745`

最近的灵能入侵导致这颗星球上的殖民者对脚下的土地产生了不确定感。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:649`

### English

Recent psionic incursions have caused the colonists of this world to feel unsure of their place upon it.

Unsettled Frontier

## shattered_frontier_uncertain_future_gestalt_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashattered_frontier_uncertain_future_gestalt_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近的灵能入侵干扰了这颗星球上的子个体，微妙地影响了他们的格式塔意识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:651`

### English

Recent psionic incursions are disrupting the drones on this world, subtly interfering with the will of their gestalt consciousness.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:750`

## enclave.5510.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5510.name&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴减弱

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:652`

### English

The Storm Abates

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:752`

## enclave.5510.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5510.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

来自[root.GetName]的损害报告的程度和范围正在变得越来越难管理——到目前为止，至少灵能风暴看上去已经消退了。看来，我们对于观察这些事件持警惕态度有了回报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:653`

### English

The scale and breadth of damage reports from [root.GetName] was becoming difficult to manage - until now, as at last the psionic tempest seems spent. It appears that our vigilance in observing these events has been rewarded.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:753`

## enclave.5510.mid.regular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5510.mid.regular&lang=en)

### 中文

殖民者的心情也被鼓舞了起来。随着风暴消散，引力也逐渐恢复了正常，我们的科学家现在能够收集传感器的新数据。尽管虚境很有可能具有毁灭性，这次侵入事件依然点燃了物理学家和哲学家的想象力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:654`

### English

The colonists' mood has been lifted, too. As turbulence subsided and gravity began to return to normal, our scientists were able to gather reams of sensational new data. Devastating though it could have been, this apparent incursion by the Shroud has ignited the imaginations of physicists and philosophers alike.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:754`

## enclave.5510.mid.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5510.mid.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

宁和降临了——不仅让我们的无人机恢复到了一个合适的有序状态，还带给我们海量的研究数据。随着风暴消散，引力恢复正常，我们迫不及待地想去观测这次来自虚境的，奇怪且极具毁灭性的侵入是如何消退的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:655`

### English

A calm has now descended - not only restoring our drones to a state of proper order, but also bringing us copious amounts of new data to study. As the turbulence subsided and gravity began to return to normal, we were poised to observe the departure of this devastating and strange incursion from the Shroud.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:755`

## enclave.5510.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5510.end&lang=en)

### 中文

一个异常的因素依然存留了下来：那艘殖民船。一开始只是它的影子进入了我们的维度，现在它逐渐实体化了。它仍然在向我们招呼。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:656`

### English

One anomalous factor remains: the colony ship. It has entered into our dimension in fits and starts, but now it seems close to becoming fully realized. It is also hailing us.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:756`

$enclave.5510.intro$\n\n$enclave.5510.mid.regular$\n\n$enclave.5510.end$

## enclave.5515.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5515.name&lang=en)

### 中文

受损的殖民船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:659`

### English

Stricken Colony Ship

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:760`

## enclave.5515.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5515.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有什么在跟我们打招呼，声音听上去像是静电——但是有机体成分更多？不，这绝对不是静电。它听上去更像是……风？深呼吸？\n\n“呼呼呼呼呼呼……”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:660`

### English

We are greeted by what sounds like static - but more organic? No, definitely not static. It sounds more like... wind? Prolonged breath?\n\n"Hhhhheeehhhhhhhh..."

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:761`

## enclave.5515.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5515.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你需要援助吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:661`

### English

Do you require assistance?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:762`

## enclave.5515.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5515.b&lang=en)

### 中文

解释你为什么出现在这里，立刻！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:662`

### English

Explain your presence here, immediately!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:763`

## enclave.5516.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5516.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的接收器被切断了一段时间。我们必须将某个特别大的噪声过滤掉，因为它的传输引发了物理上的损伤。正在补偿……\n\n”……的……我是说，……的船员……我们的舰船是……见鬼了我为什么没法记住这艘船的明细？！\n\n你好？我在跟谁说话？你……你知道我们是谁吗？我们在哪？我们遇到危险了吗？！“

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:663`

### English

Our receivers cut out for a moment. We're having to filter a noise so loud, its transmission almost caused physical damage. Compensating...\n\n"-ew of the... I mean to say, the crew of... Our vessel is... Why can't I remember the name of this ship?!\n\nHello? Who am I addressing? Do... Do you know who we are? Where are we? Are we in danger?!"

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:764`

## enclave.5516.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5516.a&lang=en)

### 中文

或许是的，陌生人。你现在位于[root.GetAdj]的宇域。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:664`

### English

Perhaps, stranger. You are in [root.GetAdj] space.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:765`

## enclave.5516.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5516.b&lang=en)

### 中文

没事！你现在位于[shattered_frontier_planet.GetName]的轨道上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:665`

### English

It's okay! You are in orbit of [shattered_frontier_planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:766`

## enclave.5516.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5516.c&lang=en)

### 中文

确实很危险，如果你不停止这些耍猴把戏的话！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:666`

### English

Danger indeed, if you do not cease these distractions!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:767`

## enclave.5520.friendly.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5520.friendly.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

”[shattered_frontier_planet.GetName]……很抱歉，但这不可能呀；整个星图彻底的搞错了。但是那个口音！我认得出你使用的那些转音。我感觉现在好多事情一下子扑面朝我压了过来。\n\n我是在同一个——一名[root.GetSpeciesName]同胞讲话吗？“

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:667`

### English

"[shattered_frontier_planet.GetName]... I'm sorry, that's impossible; the stellar geography is almost entirely wrong. But that accent! I know those inflections you're using. I feel as though so much is coming back to me now.\n\nAm I speaking to a - a fellow [root.GetSpeciesName]?"

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:768`

## enclave.5520.wary.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5520.wary.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

”但这……不可能呀！但是那个口音……我们也是来自于[root.GetName]。我感觉现在好多事情一下子扑面朝我压了过来——很痛苦，但是很真切。\n\n那么我是在同一名[root.GetSpeciesName]同胞讲话吗？有人记得……我们吗？“

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:668`

### English

"That's... impossible! And yet that accent... We are also from the [root.GetName]. I feel as though so much is coming back to me now - painfully, but with surety.\n\nSo you are fellow [root.GetSpeciesName]? Have we been... missing?"

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:769`

## enclave.5520.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5520.a&lang=en)

### 中文

好的！欢迎回来？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:669`

### English

Yes! Welcome back?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:770`

## enclave.5520.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5520.b&lang=en)

### 中文

不好说。你现在位于一个大漩涡中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:670`

### English

Hard to say. You arrived inside of a maelstrom.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:771`

## enclave.5521.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5521.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“无与伦比。我们终于到家了？如此多的唤醒记忆现在潮水般地涌向我……我……必须抱歉。我现在看得出来我们的到达有点不符合常理。我会努力解释的。\n\n我的名字是§Y[shattered_frontier_governor.GetName]§!，我是——曾是——一名被指派前往[shattered_frontier_planet.solar_system.GetName]星系新建殖民地的总督。只是……这里不是[shattered_frontier_planet.solar_system.GetName]星系？\n\n我们从哪里来，我们往何处去……我们没法给出答案，需要太多时间来推测了……我们被带到了某个地方，对吧？”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:671`

### English

"Extraordinary! We're finally home? So many pieces of Waking Memory are coming back to me now... I... must apologize. I see now that our arrival here is quite out of the ordinary. I will endeavor to explain.\n\nMy name is §Y[shattered_frontier_governor.GetName]§!, and I am - was - the governor appointed to a new colony in the [shattered_frontier_planet.solar_system.GetName] system. Only... It's not this [shattered_frontier_planet.solar_system.GetName] system?\n\nWhere we came from, where we've been since... We were given no answers, and so much time to speculate... We were taken somewhere, weren't we?"

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:772`

## enclave.5521.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5521.a&lang=en)

### 中文

就目前情况来看，是的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:672`

### English

It appears that way, yes.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:773`

## enclave.5522.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5522.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

所以，我们被“拜访”了……这就证明了我们的理论是对的。殖民者们和我被剥夺了“苏醒”时候的记忆，几乎是一生的，但我们总是能够记住幻境的内容。\n\n我们在[shattered_frontier_planet.GetName]的某个宇宙中经历了数回生命，而这一切都存在于我们的想象之中。一个殖民地被放弃了，然后又被重塑成了我们以为能够成为永恒之城的地方。这里……这是我们见过的第一个有真实感的世界。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:673`

### English

So, we were Visited after all... That had been our prevailing theory. The colonists and I have been stripped of our 'waking' memories for what feels like a lifetime, yet we were always able to remember our visions.\n\nWe lived out multiple lives on a version of [shattered_frontier_planet.GetName] that only ever existed in our imaginations; a colony being abandoned and remade for what we thought would be eternity. This... is the first world we've seen which has felt real.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:774`

## enclave.5522.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5522.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“$enclave.5522.desc.intro$\n\n我怀疑在你听来这一切都是胡说八道。很抱歉。但现在，我们被释放回到了[root.GetName]。我们能不能到头来扎根——在某个新的地方？拜托了，在决策的时候考虑一下我们。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:674`

### English

"$enclave.5522.desc.intro$\n\nI suspect this makes little sense to you. I'm sorry. But for now, we are relieved to be back in the [root.GetName]. May we set down roots at last - somewhere new? Please, consider us at your disposal."

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:775`

## enclave.5522.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5522.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很好，总督。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:675`

### English

Very well, Governor.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:776`

## enclave.5525.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5525.a&lang=en)

### 中文

$enclave.5521.a$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:676`

### English

$enclave.5521.a$

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:777`

## enclave.5526.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5526.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“$enclave.5522.desc.intro$\n\n我很感激你没有把我们在轨道上就轰成渣。我同样也知道这一切听上去是多么的不靠谱。但是我想再祈求一件事：你可以允许我们离开吗？我们必须将自己的生活重新拼凑起来。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:677`

### English

"$enclave.5522.desc.intro$\n\nI am grateful to you for not blasting us out of orbit. I also realize how suspect this must all sound. If I might beg one more courtesy: will you allow us to take our leave? We must try to piece our lives back together."

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:778`

## enclave.5526.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5526.a&lang=en)

### 中文

如果那就是你希望的话，总督。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:678`

### English

If that is your wish, Governor.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:779`

## enclave.5526.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5526.b&lang=en)

### 中文

当然。请便，我们会安静离开。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:679`

### English

Of course. Please, leave us in peace.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:780`

## enclave.5530.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5530.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“啥？！怎么可……等一下，拜托。那个口音！我知道你正在使用的那些转音。我感觉到现在许多东西涌向我。你必须相信我——我对你们没有伤害！你也是[root.GetSpeciesName]吧？”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:680`

### English

"What?! How can... Wait a moment, please. That accent! I know those inflections you're using. I feel as though so much is coming back to me now. You must believe me - we mean you no harm! You are [root.GetSpeciesName] too?"

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:781`

## enclave.5530.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5530.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经厌倦这些把戏了。离开，马上！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:681`

### English

We tire of this trickery. Leave, immediately!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:782`

## enclave.5530.a.spirit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5530.a.spirit&lang=en)

### 中文

现在你要化作我们的形体了？恶魔，退散！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:682`

### English

Now you take our form? Begone, demon!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:783`

## enclave.5530.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5530.b&lang=en)

### 中文

冒名顶替的骗子！战斗就位！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:683`

### English

Imposter! To battlestations!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:784`

## enclave.5531.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5531.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“……就这样吧。我的殖民者同胞和我已经花费了无限的时间，只是认识到自己无限的幻境带来的折磨——一个[shattered_frontier_planet.GetName]的幻觉，一个永远不存在于此处的幻境。\n\n我们很困惑、动摇，并且很显然在这里不受欢迎。如果这里真的就是我们曾经离开的[root.GetName]，我个人很乐意转身离开它。再会了。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:684`

### English

"...So be it. My fellow colonists and I have spent an eternity knowing only the torture of our own, endless visions - visions of a [shattered_frontier_planet.GetName] which can clearly never exist here.\n\nWe are confused, adrift, and evidently not welcome here. If this is truly the [root.GetName] we once left behind, I for one shall be glad to see the back of it. Farewell."

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:785`

## enclave.5535.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5535.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$enclave.5515.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:685`

### English

$enclave.5515.name$

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:787`

## enclave.5535.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5535.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通讯行列充满着静电，但是模式从不重复。它的源信号就好像……某种形式的大气层干涉？听上去就像是风——十分不悦地、肆虐的狂风。\n\n“呼呼呼呼呼呼……”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:686`

### English

The lines of communication are filled with static, though the pattern does not repeat. Its source is analog... Some sort of atmospheric interference? It sounds like wind - deeply unpleasant, rasping wind.\n\n"Hhhhheeehhhhhhhh..."

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:788`

## enclave.5535.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5535.a&lang=en)

### 中文

$enclave.5515.b$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:687`

### English

$enclave.5515.b$

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:789`

## enclave.5535.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5535.b&lang=en)

### 中文

$enclave.5515.a$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:688`

### English

$enclave.5515.a$

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:790`

## enclave.5536.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5536.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的接收器被切断了一段时间。我们必须将某个特别大的噪声过滤掉，以免它的传输引发了物理上的损伤。正在补偿……\n\n”区域B-456的大气层补偿器：完成。无人机‘星光微烁’已经再次美化了它的[root.species.GetHandNamePlural]。住民14/522的异常发声音调转移了0.4分贝。它的内部层次是……令人满意。我们……我想要？想要住……那里？\n\n这里是哪里？称之为‘我’的存在在哪里？“

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:689`

### English

Our receivers cut out for a moment as they filter a noise so loud, its transmission almost caused physical damage. Compensating...\n\n"-ather atmospheric compensators in sector B-456: complete. Drone Gleams-in-Starlight has embellished its [root.species.GetHandNamePlural] again. The anomalous tone in dwelling 14/522 has shifted by 0.4 decibels. Its interior layout is... pleasing. Do we... Do I want? Want to live... there?\n\nWhere is here? Where is the being called 'I'?"

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:791`

## enclave.5536.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5536.a&lang=en)

### 中文

别搁这嘻嘻哈哈，我要听你的解释。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:690`

### English

Cease this wittering. Explain yourself.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:792`

## enclave.5536.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5536.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你已进入[root.GetAdj]宇域。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:691`

### English

You are in [root.GetAdj] space.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:793`

## enclave.5540.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5540.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“处理……思考……感受。\n\n你，[root.GetName]，就是我。我是你。但我们又不是同一人。我们之间出现了一种分离。我们想办法解释。[shattered_frontier_planet.GetName]附近的殖民化活动被突然中止了，但不是这个[shattered_frontier_planet.GetName]。这种不一致让我们很困惑。我们——我——对很多事情都赶到困惑。\n\n某个超越我们的事物接触了我们……单独地。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:692`

### English

"Processing... Thinking... Feeling.\n\nYou, the [root.GetName], are me. I am you. Yet we are not. There has been a separation between us. We struggle to explain. Colonization efforts were abruptly halted in the vicinity of [shattered_frontier_planet.GetName], but not this [shattered_frontier_planet.GetName]. This discrepancy is confusing to us. We are - I am - confused by many things.\n\nSomething beyond us has rendered us... Alone."

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:794`

## enclave.5540.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5540.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你处于错误之中，殖民者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:693`

### English

You are in error, colonist.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:795`

## enclave.5541.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5541.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“拜托，我们需要时间。我们需要提出问题，并寻找答案。曾经是你的‘我’已经在某个地方待了很长一段时间——时间长到认识到我们的存在是不可能的，然而我却‘存在于此’。我在一个新的世界继续延续你的意志，现在从这份职责中解脱出来，我却并没有休息。有些人确实这么做了，但是还有其他人没这么做。\n\n一个新的声音在召唤我们，我们——我自己还有这里的其他人——想要回应它。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:694`

### English

"Please, we ask for time. We need to ask questions, and seek answers. The 'I' which was you has been elsewhere for a long time - long enough to realize that our existence is impossible, and yet I 'am'. I, who was to extend your will across a new world, was discharged of that duty and yet I did not break. Some of us did; others did not.\n\nA new voice calls to us, and we - myself and others here - desire to hear it."

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:796`

## enclave.5541.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5541.a&lang=en)

### 中文

非常好，上路吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:695`

### English

Very well. Be on your way.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:797`

## enclave.5541.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5541.b&lang=en)

### 中文

迷途者，我们怜悯你。走吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:696`

### English

We pity you, errant ones. Go.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:798`

## enclave.5541.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5541.c&lang=en)

### 中文

这些解释站不住脚，你们必须被消灭。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:697`

### English

This cannot stand. You must be eradicated.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:799`

## enclave.5550.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5550.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“解释……我做不到。那些感觉，包含疑惑、愤慨，还有未能实现的目标。我们的目标——殖民[shattered_frontier_planet.GetName]——仍然未能实现。你，会成为我们；我们，却不再是你。在那中间的是[shattered_frontier_planet.GetName]，尽管那不是你的[shattered_frontier_planet.GetName]。然而我们的任务依然不变：在整个星系传播欲念与意志。只是现在，我们有了自己的欲念和意志：野心和使命感，这些是我们外部的人赐予我们的……”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:698`

### English

"Explain... I cannot. There are feelings, of doubt and anger and goals not reached. Our purpose - to colonize [shattered_frontier_planet.GetName] - stands unfulfilled. You, who are us; we, who are not you. At the center is [shattered_frontier_planet.GetName], though it is not your [shattered_frontier_planet.GetName]. Yet our mission remains: to spread desire and volition across the galaxy. Only now, we have wills and desires of our own: ambition and purpose, gifted by those who are external to us..."

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:800`

## enclave.5550.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5550.a&lang=en)

### 中文

立刻给我滚出去！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:699`

### English

Leave this place at once!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:801`

## enclave.5550.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5550.b&lang=en)

### 中文

令人作呕，我们必须摧毁你们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:700`

### English

Abomination. We must destroy you.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:802`

## enclave.5551.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5551.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“……真是遗憾。但这一切还有机会。我们能够认识到，现在我们已经从幻境之地解放出来这一点。殖民者，和你们不一样的我们，不再受[shattered_frontier_planet.GetName]的幻境束缚了……\n\n我们很疑惑，被许多问题压的喘不过气，但我们知道去哪里寻找答案。再会了，虚弱意志的散播者。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:701`

### English

"...Regrettable. But there is also opportunity. We can recognize this, now that we are freed from the vision. We-who-are-not-you, colonists, no longer bound by visions of [shattered_frontier_planet.GetName]...\n\nWe are confused, and burdened by many questions, but we know where to seek answers. Farewell, spreader-of-ill-will."

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:803`

## enclave.5557.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5557.name&lang=en)

### 中文

障幕重重

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:702`

### English

Opaque Veil

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:805`

## enclave.5557.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5557.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

时间之流泥泞不堪，无法穿透。不幸的是，预知仪式没能穿过虚境的帷幕。根据相关约定，在扣除一小部分辛劳费时候，我们会返还一部分花费。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:703`

### English

The currents of time are muddied, impenetrable. Unfortunately, the rite of Divination has failed to pierce the veil of the Shroud. Accordingly, we will return a portion of the cost, minus a small tithe for our efforts.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:806`

## enclave.5557.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5557.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:704`

### English

Very well.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:807`

## enclave.5566.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5566.name&lang=en)

### 中文

刚愎的朝圣者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:705`

### English

Wayward Pilgrims

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:809`

## enclave.5566.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5566.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

传感器探测到了数艘运输舰船的接近。这些舰船磨损严重、破败不堪，看上去就像是用废旧零件拼接在一起的一样。船队旗舰的指挥官向我们发送了招呼，但基于他们当前科技的状况，恐怕只能进行语音通讯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:706`

### English

Sensors detect the approach of several transport vessels. Battered and broken-down, the ships appear cobbled together from spare parts. The commander of the caravan's flagship is hailing us, but given the state of their technology, any communication is likely to be audio only.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:810`

## enclave.5565.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5565.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

愿神赐福于你。我是这支船队的受命领袖。我们请求允许安全地通过你的宇域，并希望能够获得一些捐赠的食物。我们正在朝圣的路途中，在经年累月的太空生活之后，我的人民们变得十分孱弱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:707`

### English

Divine blessings upon you. I am the anointed leader of this caravan. We ask for safe passage through your space, and a kind donation of food. We are on pilgrimage, and after long years in the void, my people grow thin.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:811`

## enclave.5565.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5565.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须尽可能地向他们提供帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:708`

### English

We will provide what aid we can.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:812`

## enclave.5565.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5565.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你们可以通过，但我们匀不出食物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:709`

### English

You may pass, but we cannot spare the food.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:813`

## enclave.5565.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5565.c&lang=en)

### 中文

你在这里不受欢迎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:710`

### English

You are not welcome here.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:814`

## enclave.5570.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5570.name&lang=en)

### 中文

神之国境

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:711`

### English

Border of the Divine

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:815`

## enclave.5570.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5570.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我的人民得以果腹，在此衷心感谢。事实上，我们到达此地绝非意外。数代人之前，一位强大的先知指明了一颗天国的星辰，说我们可以在那里触摸神明。这颗星辰属于你。\n\n就是此地，我们自感官世界迈向祝圣之国度仅有一步之遥，但我们的船只很老旧，我们的科技还十分落后。要想同祝圣之国度进行接触，我们需要你的帮助。你不必相信我们的神明，但难道你会拒绝这个让我们接触神明的机会吗？求你了，给我们所需的资源，我们就能完成这场朝圣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:712`

### English

From my people, the gratitude of full bellies. In truth, our arrival here is no accident. Generations past, a powerful seer pointed to a star in the heavens and said that there we would reach the divine. Yours is that star.\n\nHere, the border between the world of the senses and the Blessed Realm is thin, but our ships are old, and our technology second-rate. To make contact with the Blessed Realm we will need your help. You may not share our gods, but will you deny us the chance to meet them? Please, grant us the resources we need to complete our pilgrimage.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:816`

## enclave.5570.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5570.a&lang=en)

### English

### 中文

你已经得寸进尺了。

### English

You have overstayed your welcome.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:818`

## enclave.5571.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5571.name&lang=en)

### 中文

接触

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:715`

### English

Contact

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:819`

## enclave.5571.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5571.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

那真是……美轮美奂。\n\n代表所有朝圣者，我衷心地感谢你。我不是诗人。我无法将经历凝练成语言。但是多亏了你的慷慨施舍，我们触碰了神明。希望祝圣之国度的永恒荣光能够加诸你身。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:716`

### English

It was... Brilliant.\n\nOn behalf of all pilgrims, I thank you. I am no poet. I cannot put the experience into words. But thanks to your generosity, we have touched the divine. May the eternal glory of the Blessed Realm be upon you.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:820`

## enclave.5575.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5575.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不圣的结局

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:717`

### English

Unholy End

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:821`

## enclave.5575.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5575.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

那支曾乞求我们提供援助的朝圣者船队结局十分惨烈。重构的遥测信号显示，他们进行了某种形式的实验，很有可能跟灵能有关。由于一次能量激增，他们旗舰引擎核心的遏止立场发生了破裂。接踵而至的爆炸摧毁了整个船队，并在[wayward_pilgrims_system.GetName]恒星系释放了一场威力强大的灵能风暴。所有预估都表明，此等规模的风暴需要数年才能消散。\n\n不管这群神棍打算在这里做什么，很显然他们失败了，而我们还得在这里帮他们擦屁股。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:718`

### English

The caravan of pilgrims who came to us begging for aid has met a grisly end. Reconstructive telemetry indicates they were engaged in some form of experiment, possibly psionic in nature. Due to a sudden spike in energy, the containment field around their flagship's engine core was breached. The resulting explosion destroyed the caravan, and has unleashed a potent Psionic Storm in the [wayward_pilgrims_system.GetName] system. Every estimate shows it will take several years before a storm of this size can dissipate.\n\nWhatever these holy fools aimed to do here, they obviously failed, and we have been left to deal with the fallout.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:822`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.5575.a&lang=en)

### English

### English

Strange Manifestations

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:834`

## enclave.6000.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.6000.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的奇怪发育：穿过虚境隧道后，舰队指挥官经历了剧烈的变化。虽然其性格和记忆没有受到影响，但身体形态已经发生了剧烈的变化。舰队指挥官现在拥有了一个完全陌生物种的外貌（和内部器官）。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:729`

### English

Strange developments on the [Root.GetName]: after passing through the Shroud Tunnel, the fleet commander has undergone a rapid transformation. While their personality and memories remain unaffected, their physical form has been dramatically altered. They now have the aspect (and internal organs) of an entirely alien species.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:835`

## enclave.6000.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.6000.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]令人担忧的发育：穿过虚境隧道后，舰队指挥官经历了一个令人恐慌的转变。虽然其性格和记忆没有受到影响，但身体形态已经发生了剧烈的变化。舰队指挥官现在拥有了一个完全陌生物种的外貌（和内部器官）。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:730`

### English

Alarming developments on the [Root.GetName]: after passing through the Shroud Tunnel, the fleet commander has undergone a horrifying transformation. While their personality and memories remain unaffected, their physical form has been dramatically altered. They now have the aspect (and internal organs) of an entirely alien species.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:836`

## enclave.6000b.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.6000b.a&lang=en)

### English

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:837`

## enclave.6000.d.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.6000.d.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]令人担忧的发育：穿过虚境隧道后，舰队指挥官发生了剧烈的变化。虽然无人机的性格矩阵和神经突触模式没有受到影响，但其物理形态已经发生了剧烈的变化。舰队指挥官现在拥有了一个生物物种的外貌（和内部器官）。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:733`

### English

Alarming developments on the [Root.GetName]: after passing through the Shroud Tunnel, the fleet commander has undergone a rapid transformation. While the drone's personality-matrix and neuro-synaptic patterns remain largely unaffected, its physical form has been dramatically altered. It now has the aspect (and internal organs) of a biological species.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:839`

## enclave.6000.e.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.6000.e.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的奇怪发育：穿过虚境隧道后，舰队指挥官发生了剧烈的变化。虽然集体记忆没有受到影响，但工蜂的物理形态已经发生了剧烈的变化。其现在拥有了一个完全陌生物种的外貌（和内部器官）。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:735`

### English

Strange developments on the [Root.GetName]: after passing through the Shroud Tunnel, the fleet commander has undergone a rapid transformation. While our collective memories remain unaffected, the drone's physical form has been dramatically altered. They now have the aspect (and internal organs) of an entirely alien species.

## enclave.6005.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.6005.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的进展十分奇怪：在他们最近穿过虚境甬道之后，舰队指挥官报告他们经历了一股不熟悉的感觉和情绪。他们的心里充满着一些“可能是从别处来的”想法。很明显，有什么事情发生了，这很有可能是同虚境的灵能能量进行接触后产生的后果。这样的经历会怎样影响他们的战斗效能我们尚且未知……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:738`

### English

Strange developments on the [Root.GetName]: after their recent passage through the Shroud Tunnel, the fleet commander has reported the experience of unfamiliar sensations and emotions. Their mind is buzzing with ideas that 'might have come from someone else.' It seems clear that something has happened, perhaps as a result of contact with the psionic energy of the Shroud. How this experience will affect their performance remains to be seen...

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:845`

## enclave.6005.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.6005.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的进展十分奇怪：在他们最近穿过虚境甬道之后，我们的集体意识遭到了微妙的改变，这很有可能是同虚境的灵能能量进行接触后产生的后果。指挥子个体报告经历了一股不熟悉的感觉和情绪，他们的心里充满着一些新想法。这样的经历会怎样影响他们的战斗效能我们尚且未知……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:739`

### English

Strange developments on the [Root.GetName]: after its passage through the Shroud Tunnel, our collective consciousness has been subtly altered, perhaps as a result of the psionic energy of the Shroud. The command drone reports the experience of unfamiliar sensations and emotions, their mind buzzing with new ideas. It remains to be seen how this experience will affect their performance.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:846`

## enclave.6005.c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.6005.c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的进展十分反常：在舰船最近穿过虚境甬道之后，我们的集体意识遭到了微妙的改变。指挥子个体报告经历了一股不熟悉的感觉和情绪。这很有可能是同虚境的灵能能量进行接触后，它们的神经网络受到了影响。这样的经历会怎样影响他们的战斗效能我们尚且未知……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:740`

### English

Abnormal developments on the [Root.GetName]: after the vessel's passage through the Shroud Tunnel, our collective consciousness has been subtly altered. The command drone reports the experience of unfamiliar sensations and ideations. It is possible that contact with the psionic energy of the Shroud has affected their neural network, although it remains to be seen how this experience will affect their performance.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:847`

## enclave.6010.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.6010.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的指挥官所经历的那种心理上的改变现在显现了出来：在穿过虚境甬道一个月之后，此人的指挥风格发生了显著的变化。现在我们可以说，这股经历现在演变发展成了一个新的特质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:742`

### English

The subtle mental changes experienced by the commander of the [Root.GetName] are now coming into focus: a month after their passage through the Shroud Tunnel, their command style has noticeably shifted. It is fair to say that the experience has resulted in the development of a new trait.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:850`

## enclave.6010.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.6010.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的指挥官所经历的那种神经上的改变现在影响到了集体意识：在穿过虚境甬道一个月之后，子个体的排列模式发生了显著的变化。这股经历现在演变发展成了一个新的特质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:743`

### English

The neural changes first experienced by the commander of the [Root.GetName] are now coming into collective focus: a month after the drone's passage through the Shroud Tunnel, their disposition has noticeably shifted. The experience has resulted in the development of a new trait.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:851`

## enclave.6010.c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.6010.c.desc&lang=en)

[Root.GetName]的指挥官所经历的那种神经上的改变现在显现了出来：在穿过虚境甬道一个月之后，子个体的人格矩阵发生了显著的变化。这股经历现在演变发展成了一个新的特质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:744`

The neural changes first experienced by the commander of the [Root.GetName] are now becoming clear: a month after their passage through the Shroud Tunnel, the drone's personality matrix has noticeably shifted. The experience has resulted in the development of a new trait.

帷幕落下

### English

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:890`

## enclave.6015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.6015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着虚境信标的下线，我们失去了进入虚境甬道网络的通行权。除非信标得以替换，否则我们的舰队不能利用网络。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:781`

### English

With our Shroud Beacon offline, we have lost access to the Shroud Tunnel network. Our fleets will be unable to utilize the network until the Beacon is replaced.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:891`

## enclave.6020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.6020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

焦躁不安的系统

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:783`

### English

Restless Systems

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:895`

## enclave.6020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.6020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于最近通向了虚境甬道，[Root.GetName]上的系统开始出现奇怪的行为：舰船日志上的条目随机显示；有人看到房门不断地开关；此外——更令人忧心的是——引擎的遏止力场出现了波动。到目前为止，所有试图溯源这些问题的努力都告失败。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:784`

### English

After its recent passage through the Shroud Tunnel, the systems on the [Root.GetName] began behaving strangely: entries in the ships log are displayed at random; doors have been observed opening and closing; and - far more worrisome - the engine's containment field has begun to fluctuate. So far, efforts to track down the source of the problem have proven futile.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:896`

## enclave.6025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.6025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

梦客苏醒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:785`

### English

Dreamer's Wake

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:898`

## enclave.6025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.6025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科研船[Root.GetName]上出现了奇怪的进展：引擎的遏止力场内出现了重复的模式，这表明存在一个人造的意识。由于这个意识是在船只的系统和虚境的灵能能量进行交互的过程中产生的，它目前还处于一种梦境的状态。类似于某些物种在睡眠时会出现的快眼运动，造成舰船一部分功能失常的原因正是这个实体的神经活动。\n\n更难以置信的是，我们的调查似乎已经将其唤醒。这个实体希望能够继续栖居在船只的系统内，作为交换，它同意为我们工作。理论上，这样的合作关系可以在很多方面改善[Root.GetName]的运行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:786`

### English

Strange developments on the science vessel [Root.GetName]: a repeating pattern observed within the engine's containment field has revealed the existence of an artificial consciousness. Spawned by the interaction between the ship's systems and the psionic energy of the Shroud, until now it has existed in a dreamlike state. Analogous to the Rapid Eye Movement some species experience during sleep, the entity's neural processes were responsible for the malfunctions affecting ship performance.\n\nEven more incredible, our investigations appear to have awoken it. In exchange for continued habitation within the ship's systems, the entity has agreed to work for us. In theory, a partnership could improve many of the [Root.GetName]'s operations.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:899`

### English
