## enclave.7000.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7000.title&lang=en)

### 中文

[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:794`

### English

[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:908`

## enclave.7000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

各位来客，我们在此向你问好。\n\n我们早已知晓你们的存在，但由于担心自己会给你们的未来带来不恰当的影响，我们一直躲在幕后。不过，我们现在是时候从垂帘后走出来了，对于外行人而言，虚境的运作机制可能显得古怪且令人不安。正是处于这个原因，我们决定为你们提供指导。如果你们同意的话，我们愿意成为你们的引路人。\n\n去探索吧，不过在我们下次重逢之前，记得注意安全。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:795`

### English

Fellow travelers, we greet you.\n\nYour presence on the galactic stage has long been known to us, but until now, afraid we might unduly influence your destiny, we have held back. However, the time has come to lift the veil; the ways of the Shroud may seem strange and unnerving to the uninitiated, and it is for this reason that we offer guidance. Realize that you have someone willing to be your teacher, if you so wish.\n\nWander safely, until our next encounter.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:909`

## enclave.7000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

有趣……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:796`

### English

Interesting...

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:910`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7005.title&lang=en)

### 中文

[salvager_enclave_country.GetName]

### English

[salvager_enclave_country.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:912`

## enclave.7005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自从[Root.GetName]一些流言传到我们耳朵里已经有一段时间了，我们很高兴能够与您建立联系。\n\n为了赚取微薄的利润，我们，[salvager_enclave_country.GetName]，目前向外提供特别服务，像是什么引擎升级，拆解舰船什么的，不管你有什么需求，我们都可以满足！\n\n为了我们一开始就受到您的青睐，我们可以送您一件小礼物来表示我们对于为客户服务的真心，您看怎么样？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:798`

### English

We've been hearing rumors about the impressive [Root.GetName] for a while now, and we're pleased to make your acquaintance!\n\nFor a nominal contribution of credits, we of the [salvager_enclave_country.GetName] offer premium salvager services. From engine upgrades to scrapping fleets, whatever your needs, you can count on us!\n\nAnd to ensure that we start off in your good graces, how about a small gift to show our commitment to customer service?

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:913`

## enclave.7005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

感谢你们送出的礼物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:799`

### English

We thank you for the gift.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:914`

## enclave.7005.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7005.b&lang=en)

### 中文

异种渣滓，送个小礼物本来就是你份内的事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:800`

### English

A gift is only to be expected, xeno.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:915`

## shroudwalker_insight_cooldown_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashroudwalker_insight_cooldown_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§R虚境行者在§Y[root.shroudwalker_insight_timer]§!之前无法再次尝试窥得另一份§Y洞见§!。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:802`

### English

§RThe Shroudwalkers will not be able to attempt another §YInsight§! until §Y[root.shroudwalker_insight_timer]§!.§!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:918`

## enclave.7050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对于我们来说有句俗话：‘秘密在梦中翩翩起舞。’对于虚境来说，没有哪个堡垒是不可突破的，没有什么密码是破解不了的。通过我们的冥想，其他人坚信深藏不露的东西会乖乖呈现在我们面前。你有兴趣了解你身边的人在密谋些什么吗？去了解他们的设想，就好像那设想就是你的东西一样？如果你有所求，我们会分享自己的见解。怎么利用这些就是你自己的事了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:803`

### English

A saying is known to us: 'Secrets dance in dreams.' To the Shroud, no vault is impenetrable, no code unbreakable. In our meditations, what others believe hidden is often laid bare. Would it interest you to learn what your peers are plotting? To know their designs as if they were your own? Should you wish it, we will share our insights. How you make use of them is up to you.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:920`

## enclave.7055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境在波动；它的帷幕变得轻薄。我们将从冥想中退出来。我们窥得的所有见解都会对你公开。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:810`

### English

The Shroud wavers; its veil grows thin. We will now retire to our meditations. Whatever insights we glean will be made known to you.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:928`

## enclave.7060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

据说，在虚境内每一个心灵都会投下自己的影子。通过我们的冥想，某个特定心灵的踟蹰不定在我们面前展露无遗，此人倍受信赖，但其忠诚心却不如看上去的那番坚定。备份已经交给你了。毫无疑问这个人将会成为一名心甘情愿的线人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:811`

### English

It is said that within the Shroud every mind casts its shadow. During our meditations, the wavering of a certain mind was laid bare to us, a trusted voice whose loyalty is not as steadfast as it appears. Their duplicity is your gain. No doubt they will prove a willing asset.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:930`

## enclave.7060.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7060.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§H[from.GetName]§!的间谍网获得了一位§Y新线人§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:812`

### English

We have acquired a §Ynew Asset§! for our Spy Network in the §H[from.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:931`

## enclave.7065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在你的麾下有许多位身怀绝技的特工，但每一位都困于自身的限制。任何一双眼睛都不能看到自己。但虚境却没有这样的限制；我们的冥想能够揭示许多事情。如果你希望自己的秘密行动能够马到成功，那么你最好是多多聆听我们的建议。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:813`

### English

You have many skilled agents in your employ, but each is bound by their own limitations. An eye cannot perceive itself. The Shroud knows no such boundaries; our meditations have revealed much. If your clandestine operations are to thrive, you would do well to heed our advice.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:933`

## enclave.7065.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7065.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

提高我们在§H[from.GetName]§!间谍网的§Y渗透等级§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:814`

### English

Increased our Spy Network §YInfiltration Level§! in the §H[from.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:934`

## enclave.7070.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7070.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

凡世间的国度并不如其看上去那么坚固。那些居住在里面的人也是这样。你身边人的秘密都展现在了我们面前。我们了解到的事情现在都交给你了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:815`

### English

The corporeal realm is not as solid as it appears. So it is with those who dwell within. The secrets of your peers have been revealed to us. What we have learned is now yours.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:936`

## enclave.7070.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7070.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

增加我们在§H[from.GetName]§!的§Y情报§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:816`

### English

## enclave.7075.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7075.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们有句老话：‘任何事情都有可能发生；这也包括无事发生这件事。’很不幸，我们的冥想没能获得任何见解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:817`

### English

A saying is known to us: 'Everything that can happen will; even nothing.' Unfortunately, our meditations have failed to produce any insight.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:939`

## enclave.7075.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7075.a&lang=en)

### 中文

那真是……令人失望。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:818`

### English

That is... a disappointment.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:940`

## shroudwalker_spy_network_modifier

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashroudwalker_spy_network_modifier&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对虚境的掌控加速了这个进程，允许我们协助[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]的任务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:819`

### English

Our mastery of the Shroud hastens the process and allows us to assist the [shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName] with their task.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:942`

## shroudwalker_spy_network_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashroudwalker_spy_network_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]会凝视虚境，以期获得对[Target.GetName]的内部运作具有指导意义的见解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:821`

### English

The [shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName] will peer into the Shroud in hopes of gaining insight into the inner workings of the [Target.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:945`

## shroudwalker_spy_network_monthly_change_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashroudwalker_spy_network_monthly_change_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

进度将主要依赖于[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]，尽管那些能够同灵能调谐的人或许能够加快进程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:823`

### English

Progress will rely mostly on the [shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName], though those with psionic attunement may be able to hasten the process.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:947`

## shroudwalker_spy_network_finish

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashroudwalker_spy_network_finish&lang=en)

### 中文

当[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]从虚境有所获得的时候，他们就会返回向我们作更新汇报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:824`

### English

The [shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName] will return with an update on what they have gleaned from the Shroud.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:948`

## shroudwalker_teacher_cooldown_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashroudwalker_teacher_cooldown_tt&lang=en)

### English

§RWe will not be able to request another teacher from the Shroudwalkers until §Y[root.shroudwalker_teacher_timer]§!.§!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:950`

## shroudwalker_teacher_already_bought

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashroudwalker_teacher_already_bought&lang=en)

### 中文

§R我们已经为虚境行者导师的效力付了钱。§!

### English

## shroudwalker_teacher_already_exists

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashroudwalker_teacher_already_exists&lang=en)

### 中文

§R这个行星已经有一位虚境行者导师在效力。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:827`

### English

## enclave.7100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

掌控虚境是一次长达10000次生死的旅途；哪怕是我们最天赋异禀的导师也只敢声称自己对虚境的神秘有一些片面的了解。即使如此，他们的智慧也是无价之宝。如果你有所求，我们可以排遣一名导师为你指点迷津。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:828`

### English

Mastery of the Shroud is a journey of 10,000 lifetimes; even our most gifted teachers can claim only partial knowledge of its mysteries. Nevertheless, their wisdom is invaluable. Should you wish it, we will dispatch one to guide your course.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:954`

## enclave.7100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们希望学习。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:829`

### English

We wish to learn.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:955`

## enclave.7105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

学海无涯苦作舟：这便是最高级的智慧。我们会给你送去一名导师。好好学习，你必将有所收获。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:830`

### English

To know that there is always more to know: this is the highest wisdom. A teacher will be sent to you. Learn well, and you will surely reap the rewards.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:957`

## enclave.7110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一段非常有趣的故事。你说的是§Y“面纱旅人”§!，对吧？在某些方面，这个虚境确实和我们自己的很像，但是对于不了解的人来说，这些差异可能很微妙；其实它们很深刻，是一种扭曲的反射，来自一个与我们不尽相同的宇宙。\n\n虚境的表面有很多裂痕。那些深入其奥秘的人最终可能并不会回到他们本来的宇宙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:831`

### English

A most interesting story. §YVeilfarer§!, you said? In some ways, this coven does resemble our own, but while these differences may appear subtle to the uninitiated, they are profound: a distorted reflection, from an universe not quite like our own.\n\nThe Shroud has many tears on its surface. Those who enter its mysteries may not always end up in the same universe from whence they started.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:959`

## enclave.7110.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7110.a&lang=en)

### 中文

跨越维度的灵能旅行……令人神往。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:832`

### English

Psionic travel across dimensions... Fascinating.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:960`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7110.a.tooltip&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:833`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:961`

## shroudwalker_teacher_explanation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashroudwalker_teacher_explanation&lang=en)

### 中文

\n我们将雇佣一位具有§Y$leader_trait_shroudwalker$§!特质的等级5行政官。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:842`

### English

\nWe will hire a Level 5 Official with the §Y$leader_trait_shroudwalker$§! Trait.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:978`

## enclave.7250.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7250.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对泽珞特别感兴趣。我们可以教你开采和储存的方法，但前提是你同意把你的产品永久地分成给我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:843`

### English

Zro is of particular interest to us. We could teach you the ways of harvesting and storing it, but only if you agree to give us a share of your production. In perpetuity.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:980`

## enclave.7250.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7250.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你们会拿到分成的。教教我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:844`

### English

You'll get your share. Teach us.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:981`

## enclave.7255.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.7255.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

泽珞是一种奇妙而又可悲的物质，有很多用途，但绝不能轻易使用，因为它是一个上古物种最后的遗产。开采了就要好好珍惜，也别忘了回报我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:845`

### English

Zro is a wonderful yet tragic substance. It has many applications, but should never be used lightly, for it is the last legacy of a most venerable species. Harvest it, treasure it, and don't forget to pay us what you owe.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:982`

## shroudwalker_zro_tithe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashroudwalker_zro_tithe&lang=en)

### English

### 中文

[shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName]教导了我们如何开采泽珞。作为回报，我们发誓要向他们交付泽珞产量的十分之一。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:847`

### English

The [shroudwalker_enclave_country.GetName] taught us how to harvest Zro. In exchange, we have sworn to give them a tenth of all our Zro production.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:985`

## enclave.8000.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.8000.start&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们对[FROM.GetName]发起进攻后，[root.GetTitle]§Y[root.GetName]§!离开了我们的国境。[root.GetHeSheCap]记录下了一段[root.GetHisHer]离开时的信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:859`

### English

Following our attack on the [FROM.GetName], [root.GetTitle] §Y[root.GetName]§! has left our borders. [root.GetHeSheCap] recorded a message upon [root.GetHisHer] departure.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:1002`

## enclave.8000.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.8000.end&lang=en)

### 中文

万幸的是，除了搬离了[root.GetHisHer]自己的工作场所以外，[root.GetName]对领导层没有造成其他任何的扰乱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:860`

### English

Thankfully, aside from vacating [root.GetHisHer] workspace, [root.GetName] did not cause any other disruptions to leadership.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:1003`

## enclave.8000.councilor_end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenclave.8000.councilor_end&lang=en)

### 中文

由于[root.GetHisHer]曾在内阁任职，[root.GetName]和[root.GetHisHer]雇员们的离开对政府运作造成了很大的干扰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:861`

### English

Due to [root.GetHisHer] involvement on the Council, the departure of [root.GetName] and [root.GetHisHer] staffers has caused major disruptions to government operations.

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:1004`

## shroudwalker_teacher_farewell

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashroudwalker_teacher_farewell&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“你已经涉足了自己无法理解的力量。谨言慎行，学会安于当下，因为在你选择的道路上，未来只会带来痛苦。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:862`

### English

§L"You have meddled with powers you cannot possibly comprehend. Tread carefully, and take what solace you can from the present, for on the path you've chosen the future holds only pain."§!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:1006`

## master_salvager_farewell

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amaster_salvager_farewell&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“行吧，你已经展露了自己的真实面目。我本有更好的期待。愿你的引擎炸响熄灭，与你一起坠入虚空。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:865`

§L"Well, you've shown your true colors. Somehow, I expected better. May your engines sputter and die. To the void with you."§!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:1010`

## curator_archivist_farewell

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acurator_archivist_farewell&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“卑鄙！庸俗！懦夫！一想到我们以为你们是文明人……但我觉得种背信弃义是意料之中的——卑劣的本能和狭隘的贪婪一直是你们民族性格的标志。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:868`

### English

§L"Despicable! Vulgar! Craven! To think that we ever considered you civilized... But I suppose such treachery was only to be expected - base instinct and petty greed ever were a mark of your people's character."§!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:1014`

## trader_leader_farewell

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrader_leader_farewell&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“那么，为什么不让我知道这个小小的市场秘密呢？告诉我，这是个什么居心？有何利可图？你们这些白痴难道不知道你毁了我们双方吗？”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/overlord_enclaves_l_simp_chinese.yml:871`

### English

§L"So why wasn't I let in on this little market secret? Enlighten me, what's the angle here? Where's the profit? Don't you idiots know you've ruined us both?"§!

Source: `localisation/english/overlord_enclaves_l_english.yml:1018`
